```ts
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import Fastify, { type FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { WebSocketServer } from 'ws';
import { rawServer, signedHeaders, tryOpen, type RawServer } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/raw-server.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import { createDoor, type DoorImpl } from '../door.js';
import { createReplayGuard } from '../replay-guard.js';
import { SocketCallError } from '../rpc.js';
import { createServerLink, type ServerLinkImpl } from '../server-link.js';

const SECRET = 's'.repeat(64);
let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
let http: FastifyInstance;
let door: DoorImpl;
let link: ServerLinkImpl;
let base: string;
const events: unknown[] = [];
// A lane's own socket (P6's media stream works the same way): it says hello and records the path it was opened on.
const laneWss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true });
const lanePaths: string[] = [];

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
  http = Fastify({ logger: false });
  link = createServerLink({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, timeoutMs: 1_000 });
  door = createDoor({
    http,
    secret: SECRET,
    guard: createReplayGuard({ pool: db.pool, now: () => new Date() }),
    link,
    log: silentLog,
    now: () => new Date(),
    pingMs: 50,
    idleMs: 300,
  });
  door.handle('send_event', async (ev) => {
    events.push(ev);
    return { accepted: true, duplicate: false };
  });
  door.handle('get_person', async (p) => {
    if (p.person_id === 'p_slow') await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2_500));
    return { person_id: p.person_id, carry_over: false, facts: [] };
  });
  door.handle('update_person', async () => {
    throw new SocketCallError(429, 'busy', 'rate_limited', 3);
  });
  door.handle('forget_person', async () => {
    throw new Error('boom');
  });
  door.handle('delete_fact', async () => ({ ok: false }) as never);
  door.onUpgrade(/^\/lane\/echo\/([a-z0-9_]+)$/, (req, socket, head) => {
    lanePaths.push(req.url ?? '');
    laneWss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => ws.send('lane hello'));
  });
  await http.listen({ port: 0, host: '127.0.0.1' });
  base = `ws://127.0.0.1:${(http.server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
});

afterAll(async () => {
  door.close();
  laneWss.close();
  await http.close();
  await db.drop();
});

let n = 0;
const target = () => `/v1/socket?instance=test-${++n}`;
const connect = () => rawServer(`${base}${target()}`, SECRET);

describe('the door', () => {
  it('GET /v1/health answers without a signature', async () => {
    const r = await http.inject({ method: 'GET', url: '/v1/health' });
    expect(r.json()).toEqual({ ok: true, contract: '1.0.0' });
  });

  it('a good signature opens the socket, and Go pings', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    await new Promise((resolve) => s.ws.once('ping', resolve));
    expect(door.openSockets()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    await s.close();
  });

  it('refuses a missing, wrong or stale signature with 401 bad_signature', async () => {
    const t = target();
    const cases = [{}, signedHeaders(t, 'x'.repeat(64)), signedHeaders(t, SECRET, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1_000) - 400)];
    for (const headers of cases) {
      const r = await tryOpen(`${base}${t}`, headers);
      expect(r).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
      if ('status' in r) expect(JSON.parse(r.body)).toMatchObject({ error: { code: 'bad_signature' } });
    }
  });

  it('accepts each signature once', async () => {
    const t = target();
    const headers = signedHeaders(t, SECRET);
    const first = await tryOpen(`${base}${t}`, headers);
    expect('ws' in first).toBe(true);
    const again = await tryOpen(`${base}${t}`, headers);
    expect(again).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    if ('status' in again) expect(JSON.parse(again.body)).toEqual({ error: { code: 'bad_signature', message: 'signature already used' } });
    // The same v1 inside a reworded header is still the same signature.
    const used = headers[C.SIGNATURE_HEADER] as string;
    for (const reworded of [`t=1,${used}`, `${used}, ${used}`, used.replace(',', ' , ')]) {
      const r = await tryOpen(`${base}${t}`, { [C.SIGNATURE_HEADER]: reworded });
      expect(r).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    }
    if ('ws' in first) first.ws.close();
  });

  it('checks the exact path and query that were signed', async () => {
    expect(await tryOpen(`${base}/v1/socket?instance=other`, signedHeaders('/v1/socket', SECRET))).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });

    const t = target();
    const r = await tryOpen(`${base}${t}`, signedHeaders(t, SECRET), { autoPong: false });
    if (!('ws' in r)) throw new Error('refused');
    await new Promise((resolve) => r.ws.once('close', resolve));
  });

  it('answers an unreadable frame with -32700 and a null id', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    s.sendRaw('not json');
    const f = await s.waitFor((x) => x.error?.code === -32700);
    expect(f.id).toBeNull();
    expect(f.error.data).toBeUndefined();
    await s.close();

  it('answers -32601 for a method Go does not know, one meant for the Server, or one with no handler yet', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    expect((await s.call('do_magic', {})).error).toEqual({ code: -32601, message: 'unknown method do_magic' });
    expect((await s.call('read_trip', { trip_id: 't_goa' })).error.code).toBe(-32601);
    expect((await s.call('catch_up', { trip_id: 't_goa', person_id: 'p_priya', since: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z' })).error.code).toBe(-32601);
    await s.close();
  });

  it('accepts an unknown event type before checking anything else', async () => {
    const before = events.length;
    const s = await connect();
    const a = await s.call('send_event', { type: 'trip.renamed', whatever: true });
    expect(a.result).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    expect(events.length).toBe(before);
    await s.close();
  });

  it('answers 422 invalid for a known event that breaks the contract', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    const a = await s.call('send_event', {
      type: 'message.created', event_id: 'ev_bad', trip_id: 't_goa', seq: 1, occurred_at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z', data: {},
    });
    expect(a.error.code).toBe(422);
    expect(a.error.data).toEqual({ code: 'invalid' });
    await s.close();
  });

  it('turns a handler failure into 500, keeps a handler\'s 429 with its wait, and refuses a bad answer', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    expect((await s.call('forget_person', { person_id: 'p_priya' })).error).toMatchObject({ code: 500, data: { code: 'internal' } });
    expect((await s.call('update_person', { person_id: 'p_priya', carry_over: true })).error).toMatchObject({
      code: 429,
      data: { code: 'rate_limited', retry_after_seconds: 3 },
    });
    expect((await s.call('delete_fact', { person_id: 'p_priya', fact_id: 'f_1' })).error.code).toBe(500);
    await s.close();
  });

  it('answers the same id twice with one answer each, running the handler once', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    const before = events.length;
    const ev = { event_id: 'ev_twice', trip_id: 't_goa', seq: 9, occurred_at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z', type: 'person.left', data: { person_id: 'p_kabir' } };
    const a = await s.call('send_event', ev, 'gs_same');
    const b = await s.call('send_event', ev, 'gs_same');
    expect(a.result).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    expect(b.result).toEqual(a.result);
    expect(events.length).toBe(before + 1);
    await s.close();
  });

  it('answers 503 inside the limit when a handler is too slow', async () => {
    const s = await connect();
    const t0 = Date.now();
    const a = await s.call('get_person', { person_id: 'p_slow' });
    expect(a.error).toMatchObject({ code: 503, data: { code: 'unavailable' } });
    expect(Date.now() - t0).toBeLessThan(2_000);
    await s.close();
  });

  it('hands the Server\'s answers to Go\'s requests to the link', async () => {
    const s = await rawServer(`${base}${target()}`, SECRET, (method) => (method === 'read_messages' ? { messages: [], has_more: false } : {}));
    await expect(link.request('read_messages', { trip_id: 't_goa', after_seq: 0 })).resolves.toEqual({ messages: [], has_more: false });
    await s.close();
  });

  it("sends Go's requests for a trip back on the socket that brought that trip's events", async () => {
    const page = { messages: [], has_more: false };
    const answer = (method: string) => (method === 'read_messages' ? page : {});
    const a = await rawServer(`${base}${target()}`, SECRET, answer);
    const b = await rawServer(`${base}${target()}`, SECRET, answer);
    const left = (tripId: string, eventId: string) => ({
      event_id: eventId, trip_id: tripId, seq: 1, occurred_at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z', type: 'person.left', data: { person_id: 'p_kabir' },
    });
    expect((await a.call('send_event', left('t_aff_a', 'ev_aff_a'))).result).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    expect((await b.call('send_event', left('t_aff_b', 'ev_aff_b'))).result).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      await expect(link.request('read_messages', { trip_id: 't_aff_a', after_seq: i })).resolves.toEqual(page);
      await expect(link.request('read_messages', { trip_id: 't_aff_b', after_seq: i })).resolves.toEqual(page);
    }
    const asked = (s: RawServer) => s.frames.filter((f) => f.method === 'read_messages').map((f) => f.params.trip_id);
    expect(asked(a)).toEqual(['t_aff_a', 't_aff_a', 't_aff_a']);
    expect(asked(b)).toEqual(['t_aff_b', 't_aff_b', 't_aff_b']);
    await a.close();
    await b.close();

});

describe('other WebSocket paths (Door.onUpgrade)', () => {
  it('a path a lane registered reaches its handler, with no signature asked by the door', async () => {
    const before = door.openSockets();
    const r = await tryOpen(`${base}/lane/echo/call_1?token=abc`, {});
    if (!('ws' in r)) throw new Error(`refused: ${r.status} ${r.body}`);

    const s = await connect();
    expect(door.openSockets()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    await s.close();
  });
});
```

`apps/brain/src/door/replay-guard.ts`:
```ts
import type pg from 'pg';

/**
 * Remembers each upgrade signature for 10 minutes, twice the 300 s skew, so it opens the socket once.
 * The key is the HMAC (v1) alone: it already binds t and the target, so a header reworded around a used
 * v1 (an extra t=, spaces, the header sent twice) is still the same signature. Memory answers first;
 * Postgres covers restarts and other copies of the service.
 */
export function createReplayGuard(o: { pool: pg.Pool; now: () => Date; keepMs?: number }) {
  const keepMs = o.keepMs ?? 10 * 60_000;
  const seen = new Map<string, number>();
  return {
    /** True the first time this signature is seen; false on any repeat. */
    async claim(t: number, v1: string): Promise<boolean> {
      if (seen.has(v1)) return false;
      seen.set(v1, o.now().getTime());
      const r = await o.pool.query('insert into seen_signatures (v1, t, seen_at) values ($1, $2, $3) on conflict do nothing', [v1, t, o.now()]);
      return r.rowCount === 1;
    },
    async prune(): Promise<void> {
      const cutoff = o.now().getTime() - keepMs;
      for (const [k, at] of seen) if (at < cutoff) seen.delete(k);
      await o.pool.query('delete from seen_signatures where seen_at < $1', [new Date(cutoff)]);
    },
  };
}
export type ReplayGuard = ReturnType<typeof createReplayGuard>;
```

`apps/brain/src/door/handlers.ts`:
```ts
import type { IncomingMessage } from 'node:http';
import type { Duplex } from 'node:stream';
import type { ToGoMethod, ToGoParams, ToGoResult } from './methods.js';
import type { ServerLink } from './server-link.js';

/** How a lane answers one of the Server's requests. */
export type ToGoHandler<M extends ToGoMethod> = (
  params: ToGoParams<M>,
  meta: { instance: string; frameId: string },
) => Promise<ToGoResult<M>>;

/** Takes over one WebSocket upgrade on Go's port: the handler checks its own auth and finishes (or refuses) the upgrade. */
export type UpgradeHandler = (req: IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer) => void;

export interface Door {
  /** One handler per method; P1 registers send_event and card_tap. A method with no handler answers -32601. */
  handle<M extends ToGoMethod>(method: M, handler: ToGoHandler<M>): void;
  /** Other WebSocket paths on Go's port (P6: Twilio's media stream at /calls/media/:callId). Checked before the /v1/socket rule. */
  onUpgrade(path: RegExp, handle: UpgradeHandler): void;
  link: ServerLink;
}
```

`apps/brain/src/door/door.ts`:
```ts
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { IncomingMessage } from 'node:http';
import type { Duplex } from 'node:stream';
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { WebSocketServer, type RawData, type WebSocket } from 'ws';
import type { z } from 'zod';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';
import type { Door, ToGoHandler, UpgradeHandler } from './handlers.js';
import { KNOWN_EVENT_TYPES, TO_GO_SCHEMAS, type ToGoMethod } from './methods.js';
import type { ReplayGuard } from './replay-guard.js';
import { errorFrame, readFrame, resultFrame, SocketCallError, type RequestFrame } from './rpc.js';
import type { ServerLinkImpl, SocketHandle } from './server-link.js';

export interface DoorOptions {
  http: FastifyInstance;
  secret: string;
  guard: ReplayGuard;
  link: ServerLinkImpl;
  log: Log;
  now: () => Date;
  pingMs?: number;
  idleMs?: number;
}

export type DoorImpl = Door & { close(): void; openSockets(): number };

class TooSlow extends Error {}

const STATUS_TEXT: Record<number, string> = { 401: 'Unauthorized', 404: 'Not Found', 503: 'Service Unavailable' };

/** Refuses an upgrade with the contract's ErrorBody; the socket never opens. */
function refuse(socket: Duplex, status: number, code: z.infer<typeof C.ErrorCode>, message: string): void {
  const body = JSON.stringify({ error: { code, message } } satisfies C.ErrorBody);
  socket.end(
    `HTTP/1.1 ${status} ${STATUS_TEXT[status] ?? 'Error'}\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\n` +
      `Content-Length: ${Buffer.byteLength(body)}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n${body}`,
  );
}

function signatureParts(header: string): { t: number; v1: string } | null {
  const fields = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const part of header.split(',')) {
    const i = part.indexOf('=');
    if (i > 0) fields.set(part.slice(0, i).trim(), part.slice(i + 1).trim());
  }
  const t = Number(fields.get('t'));
  const v1 = fields.get('v1') ?? '';
  return Number.isInteger(t) && v1 ? { t, v1 } : null;
}

function withLimit<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number): Promise<T> {
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
  const slow = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
    timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new TooSlow()), ms);
  });
  return Promise.race([p, slow]).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer));
}

function textOf(data: RawData): string {
  if (Buffer.isBuffer(data)) return data.toString('utf8');
  if (Array.isArray(data)) return Buffer.concat(data).toString('utf8');
  return Buffer.from(data).toString('utf8');
}

function send(ws: WebSocket, frame: string): void {
  if (ws.readyState === ws.OPEN) ws.send(frame);
}

export function createDoor(o: DoorOptions): DoorImpl {
  const pingMs = o.pingMs ?? C.PING_INTERVAL_SECONDS * 1_000;
  const idleMs = o.idleMs ?? C.IDLE_CLOSE_SECONDS * 1_000;
  const handlers = new Map<string, ToGoHandler<ToGoMethod>>();
  const laneUpgrades: Array<{ path: RegExp; handle: UpgradeHandler }> = [];
  const open = new Map<string, WebSocket>();
  const recent = new Map<string, Promise<string>>();
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true, maxPayload: C.MAX_FRAME_BYTES });

  o.http.get('/v1/health', async () => ({ ok: true as const, contract: C.CONTRACT_VERSION }));
  o.http.server.on('upgrade', (req: IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer) => {
    void onUpgrade(req, socket, head);
  });

  async function onUpgrade(req: IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    socket.on('error', () => socket.destroy());
    try {
      const target = req.url ?? '';
      // A lane's own path (P6's media stream) goes to its handler, which checks its own auth. The Server's socket
      // can never be one of these (onUpgrade refuses such a path), so the signature rule below always holds for it.
      const lanePath = target.split('?')[0] ?? '';
      const lane = laneUpgrades.find((u) => u.path.test(lanePath));
      if (lane) {
        lane.handle(req, socket, head);
        return;
      }
      if (target.split('?')[0] !== C.SOCKET_PATH) {
        refuse(socket, 404, 'not_found', 'no such socket');
        return;
      }
      const raw = req.headers[C.SIGNATURE_HEADER];
      const header = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
      // The signature covers the path and query exactly as sent.
      const checked = C.verifyRequest({ method: 'GET', target, body: '' }, header, o.secret, Math.floor(o.now().getTime() / 1_000));
      if (!checked.ok) {
        refuse(socket, 401, 'bad_signature', `signature ${checked.reason}`);
        return;
      }
      const parts = signatureParts(header ?? '');
      if (!parts || !(await o.guard.claim(parts.t, parts.v1))) {
        refuse(socket, 401, 'bad_signature', 'signature already used');
        return;
      }
      const instance = new URL(target, 'http://go.local').searchParams.get('instance') ?? 'default';
      wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => onSocket(ws, instance));
    } catch (e) {
      o.log.error({ err: e }, 'the socket upgrade failed');
      refuse(socket, 503, 'unavailable', 'try again in a moment');
    }
  }

  function onSocket(ws: WebSocket, instance: string): void {
    const id = `sock_${randomUUID()}`;
    let heardAt = Date.now();
    const heard = (): void => {
      heardAt = Date.now();
    };
    const handle: SocketHandle = { id, instance, send: (frame) => send(ws, frame) };
    open.set(id, ws);
    o.link.attach(handle);
    o.log.info({ instance, socket: id }, 'the Server connected');
    // Go pings every 20 s and closes a socket that has been silent for 40 s.
    const ping = setInterval(() => {
      if (ws.readyState === ws.OPEN) ws.ping();
    }, pingMs);
    const idle = setInterval(() => {
      if (Date.now() - heardAt > idleMs) ws.terminate();
    }, Math.max(10, Math.min(5_000, Math.floor(idleMs / 4))));
    ws.on('ping', heard);
    ws.on('pong', heard);
    ws.on('message', (data: RawData, isBinary: boolean) => {
      heard();
      if (isBinary) {
        send(ws, errorFrame(null, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.parse_error, 'frames are JSON text'));
        return;
      }
      void onText(ws, handle, textOf(data)).catch((e: unknown) => o.log.error({ err: e }, 'a frame failed'));
    });
    ws.on('close', () => {
      clearInterval(ping);
      clearInterval(idle);
      open.delete(id);
      o.link.detach(id);
      o.log.info({ instance, socket: id }, 'the Server disconnected');
    });
    ws.on('error', (e) => o.log.warn({ err: e, socket: id }, 'socket error'));
  }

  async function onText(ws: WebSocket, h: SocketHandle, text: string): Promise<void> {
    const f = readFrame(text);
    if (f.kind === 'unreadable') {
      send(ws, errorFrame(null, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.parse_error, `unreadable frame: ${f.reason}`));
      return;
    }
    if (f.kind === 'notification') return; // the Server sends no notifications in version 1
    if (f.kind === 'result' || f.kind === 'error') {
      o.link.onAnswer(f);
      return;
    }
    // Trip affinity: Go's requests for this trip go back on the socket that brings its events. Noted before the
    // answer, so a gap refill the event starts already goes to the right socket.
    if (f.method === 'send_event') {
      const tripId = typeof f.params === 'object' && f.params !== null ? (f.params as { trip_id?: unknown }).trip_id : undefined;
      if (typeof tripId === 'string' && tripId !== '') o.link.noteTrip(tripId, h.id);
    }
    send(ws, await answerOnce(f, h.instance));
  }

  /** One answer per request. The same id with the same content again gets the same answer; the handler runs once. */
  function answerOnce(f: RequestFrame, instance: string): Promise<string> {
    const key = createHash('sha1').update(`${f.id}\n${f.method}\n${JSON.stringify(f.params ?? null)}`).digest('hex');
    const hit = recent.get(key);
    if (hit) return hit;
    const answer = compute(f, instance);
    recent.set(key, answer);
    setTimeout(() => recent.delete(key), 5 * 60_000).unref();
    return answer;
  }

  async function compute(f: RequestFrame, instance: string): Promise<string> {
    const def = C.methodByName(f.method);
    if (!def || def.direction !== 'to_go') return errorFrame(f.id, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.unknown_method, `unknown method ${f.method}`);
    const handler = handlers.get(f.method);
    if (!handler) return errorFrame(f.id, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.unknown_method, `Go does not handle ${f.method} yet`);
    // An event type Go does not know yet is accepted before any check, so the Server can add types within version 1.
    if (f.method === 'send_event') {
      const type = typeof f.params === 'object' && f.params !== null ? (f.params as { type?: unknown }).type : undefined;
      if (typeof type === 'string' && !KNOWN_EVENT_TYPES.has(type)) return resultFrame(f.id, { accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    }
    const parsed = C.parseParams(f.method, f.params);
    if (!parsed.ok) return errorFrame(f.id, parsed.code, parsed.message);
    try {
      const result = await withLimit(handler(parsed.params as never, { instance, frameId: f.id }), (def.answer_within ?? 2) * 1_000 - 200);
      const checked = (TO_GO_SCHEMAS[f.method as ToGoMethod][1] as z.ZodType).safeParse(result);
      if (!checked.success) {
        o.log.error({ method: f.method }, 'Go built an answer outside the contract');
        return errorFrame(f.id, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.internal, `Go built a bad ${f.method} answer`);
      }
      return resultFrame(f.id, checked.data);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof SocketCallError) return errorFrame(f.id, e.code, e.message, e.retryAfterSeconds);
      if (e instanceof TooSlow) return errorFrame(f.id, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.unavailable, `Go took too long on ${f.method}`);
      o.log.error({ err: e, method: f.method }, 'a request handler failed');
      return errorFrame(f.id, C.SOCKET_ERROR_CODES.internal, 'Go failed on this request');
    }
  }

  return {
    link: o.link,
    handle<M extends ToGoMethod>(method: M, handler: ToGoHandler<M>): void {
      if (handlers.has(method)) throw new Error(`${method} already has a handler`);
      handlers.set(method, handler as unknown as ToGoHandler<ToGoMethod>);
    },
    onUpgrade(path: RegExp, handle: UpgradeHandler): void {
      // A global or sticky pattern keeps state between test() calls; a path is matched fresh every time.
      const fresh = new RegExp(path.source, path.flags.replace(/[gy]/g, ''));
      if (fresh.test(C.SOCKET_PATH)) throw new Error(`${path} would take ${C.SOCKET_PATH}, which is the Server's socket`);
      laneUpgrades.push({ path: fresh, handle });
    },
    close() {
      for (const ws of open.values()) ws.terminate();
      wss.close();
    },
    openSockets: () => open.size,
  };
}
```

```ts
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { Message, Trip, TripEvent } from '../../door/methods.js';

const person = (p: Partial<C.Person> & { person_id: string; name: string }): C.Person =>
  C.Person.parse({ role: 'member', joined_at: '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z', know: [], has_upi: false, go_paused: false, ...p });

/** The guide's Goa trip, just created: Rahul, Priya, Arjun, Meher and Kabir, nothing decided yet. */
export function goaSnapshot(over: Partial<Trip> = {}): Trip {
  return C.TripSnapshot.parse({
    trip_id: 't_goa',
    seq: 1,
    title: 'Goa 🌴',
    status: 'planning',
    created_at: '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z',
    organizer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
    invite_url: 'https://go.example/j/8fK2xQ',
    destination: { name: 'North Goa' },
    dates: { start: '2026-11-06', end: '2026-11-09' },
    vibe: ['beaches', 'food', 'sunsets'],
    stay: 'villa with a pool',
    budget: { answered: 0, of: 5 },
    people: [
      person({ person_id: 'p_rahul', name: 'Rahul', role: 'organizer', from_city: 'Delhi', earliest_leave: 'Fri after 6 pm', has_upi: true }),
      person({ person_id: 'p_priya', name: 'Priya', from_city: 'Delhi', know: ['Vegetarian', 'Window seat'] }),
      person({ person_id: 'p_arjun', name: 'Arjun', from_city: 'Bangalore', know: ['No early flights'] }),
      person({ person_id: 'p_meher', name: 'Meher', from_city: 'Mumbai', know: ['Birthday on 7 Nov'] }),
      person({ person_id: 'p_kabir', name: 'Kabir', from_city: 'Delhi', earliest_leave: 'Fri evening', has_upi: true }),
    ],
    decisions: [],
    bookings: [],
    expenses: [],
    money: { currency: 'INR', spent_minor: 0, by_category: [], transfers: [] },
    itinerary: [],
    record: [],
    ...over,
  });
}

const at = (seq: number) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 5, 10, 0, 0) + seq * 1_000).toISOString();

/** A message from a person, or from Go when personId is null, posted at change number `seq`. */
export function msg(seq: number, personId: string | null, text: string, extra: Partial<Message> = {}): Message {
  return C.Message.parse({
    message_id: `m_${seq}`,
    seq,
    updated_seq: seq,
    author: personId ? { kind: 'person', person_id: personId } : { kind: 'go' },
    text,
    attachments: [],
    mentions_go: personId !== null && /@go\b/i.test(text),
    mentions: [],
    reactions: [],
    withheld: false,
    deleted: false,
    sent_at: at(seq),
    ...extra,
  });
}

/** One event for a trip, checked against the contract. */
export function ev<T extends TripEvent['type']>(
  seq: number,
  type: T,
  data: Extract<TripEvent, { type: T }>['data'],
  tripId = 't_goa',
): Extract<TripEvent, { type: T }> {
  return C.Event.parse({ event_id: `ev_${tripId}_${seq}`, trip_id: tripId, seq, occurred_at: at(seq), type, data }) as Extract<TripEvent, { type: T }>;
}

/** A vote, open unless `over` says otherwise. */
export function vote(over: Partial<C.Decision> & { decision_id: string }): C.Decision {
  return C.Decision.parse({
    question: 'Where do we stay?',
    kind: 'choice',
    anonymous: false,
    status: 'open',
    options: [{ label: 'Villa Sol' }, { label: 'Casa Azul' }],
    voted: 0,
    of: 5,
    tally: [0, 0],
    opened_at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z',
    ...over,
  });
}
```

`apps/brain/src/__tests__/fixtures/fake-link.ts`:
```ts
import { SocketCallError } from '../../door/rpc.js';
import type { ServerLink } from '../../door/server-link.js';

export type FakeAnswers = Record<string, (params: any) => unknown>;

/** A ServerLink that answers from a map of method to function and records every call. */
export function fakeLink(answers: FakeAnswers = {}) {
  const calls: Array<{ method: string; params: any; turnId?: string }> = [];
  const notes: Array<{ method: string; params: any }> = [];
  const link: ServerLink = {
    async request(method, params, opts) {
      calls.push({ method, params, ...(opts?.turnId ? { turnId: opts.turnId } : {}) });
      const f = answers[method];
      if (!f) throw new SocketCallError(-32601, `the fake Server has no ${method}`);
      return (await f(params)) as never;
    },
    notify(method, params) {
      notes.push({ method, params });
    },
    isOpen: () => true,
  };
  return { link, calls, notes };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/log.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ev, goaSnapshot, msg } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import { UNSURE_READ } from '../../reflex/reflex.js';
import { createMessageHooks } from '../hooks.js';
import { createTripLog } from '../log.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

describe('TripLog', () => {
  it('writes each event once by event_id', async () => {
    const log = createTripLog({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
    const e = ev(1, 'trip.created', { trip: goaSnapshot() });
    expect(await log.append(e)).toBe('new');
    expect(await log.append(e)).toBe('duplicate');
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query<{ n: number }>('select count(*)::int as n from trip_events where event_id = $1', [e.event_id]);
    expect(rows[0]?.n).toBe(1);
  });

  it('knows the highest change number per trip', async () => {
    const log = createTripLog({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
    await log.append(ev(3, 'message.created', { message: msg(3, 'p_priya', 'scuba?') }));
    await log.append(ev(2, 'message.created', { message: msg(2, 'p_rahul', 'hi') }));
    expect(await log.lastSeq('t_goa')).toBe(3);
    expect(await log.lastSeq('t_nowhere')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('lanes see each event; one failing listener does not stop the others', async () => {
    const log = createTripLog({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
    const seen: string[] = [];
    log.onEvent(() => {
      throw new Error('a lane bug');
    });
    log.onEvent((e) => {
      seen.push(e.event_id);
    });
    const e = ev(4, 'person.left', { person_id: 'p_kabir' });
    await log.emit(e);
    expect(seen).toEqual([e.event_id]);
  });
});

describe('MessageHooks', () => {
  it('runs every hook in order; one failing hook does not stop the next', async () => {
    const hooks = createMessageHooks(silentLog);
    const seen: string[] = [];
    hooks.onRead(async () => {
      throw new Error('a lane bug');
    });
    hooks.onRead(async (m, read) => {
      seen.push(`${m.message_id}:${String(read.forGo.sure)}`);
    });
    await hooks.run(msg(5, 'p_priya', "I'm veg btw"), UNSURE_READ, { trip: goaSnapshot(), messages: [], seq: 5 });
    expect(seen).toEqual(['m_5:false']);
  });
});
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/pack.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fakeLink, type FakeAnswers } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/fake-link.js';
import { ev, goaSnapshot, msg, vote } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import { createPacks } from '../pack.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

function make(answers: FakeAnswers = {}) {
  const f = fakeLink(answers);
  return { packs: createPacks({ pool: db.pool, link: f.link, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() }), ...f };
}

describe('Packs', () => {
  it('loads a trip cold: read_trip, then every message page by page, oldest first', async () => {
    const T = 't_cold';
    const { packs, calls } = make({
      read_trip: () => goaSnapshot({ trip_id: T, seq: 4 }),
      read_messages: (p) =>
        p.after_seq === 0
          ? { messages: [msg(2, 'p_priya', 'hi'), msg(3, 'p_rahul', 'hello')], has_more: true }
          : { messages: [msg(4, 'p_arjun', 'yo')], has_more: false },
    });
    const pack = await packs.get(T);
    expect(pack.messages.map((m) => m.seq)).toEqual([2, 3, 4]);
    expect(pack.seq).toBe(4);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'read_messages').map((c) => c.params.after_seq)).toEqual([0, 3]);
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query<{ n: number }>('select count(*)::int as n from messages where trip_id = $1', [T]);
    expect(rows[0]?.n).toBe(3);
    await packs.get(T);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'read_trip')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(packs.active()).toContain(T);
  });

  it('makes the pack from trip.created with no read at all', async () => {
    const { packs, calls } = make();
    await packs.apply(ev(1, 'trip.created', { trip: goaSnapshot({ trip_id: 't_new' }) }, 't_new'));
    expect((await packs.get('t_new')).trip.title).toBe('Goa 🌴');
    expect(calls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('keeps the pack current from events, and reads the trip again when an event says only that it changed', async () => {
    const T = 't_live';
    const { packs, calls } = make({ read_trip: () => goaSnapshot({ trip_id: T, seq: 20, title: 'Goa trip' }) });
    const by = { kind: 'go' } as const;
    await packs.apply(ev(1, 'trip.created', { trip: goaSnapshot({ trip_id: T }) }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(2, 'message.created', { message: msg(2, 'p_priya', 'scuba sunday?') }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(3, 'reaction.added', { message_id: 'm_2', reaction: { emoji: '👍', person_id: 'p_rahul' } }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(4, 'reaction.added', { message_id: 'm_2', reaction: { emoji: '👍', person_id: 'p_rahul' } }, T));
    await packs.apply(
      ev(5, 'person.joined', {
        person: { person_id: 'p_zoya', name: 'Zoya', role: 'member', joined_at: '2026-10-05T11:00:00Z', know: [], has_upi: false, go_paused: false },
      }, T),
    );
    await packs.apply(ev(6, 'decision.opened', { decision: vote({ decision_id: 'd_stay' }), by }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(7, 'decision.voted', { decision_id: 'd_stay', person_id: 'p_priya', option_index: 0, voted: 1, of: 6, tally: [1, 0] }, T));
    let pack = await packs.get(T);
    expect(pack.messages[0]?.reactions).toEqual([{ emoji: '👍', person_id: 'p_rahul' }]);
    expect(pack.trip.people.map((p) => p.person_id)).toContain('p_zoya');
    expect(pack.trip.decisions[0]).toMatchObject({ voted: 1, tally: [1, 0], votes: [{ person_id: 'p_priya', option_index: 0 }] });

    await packs.apply(
      ev(8, 'decision.closed', {
        decision: vote({ decision_id: 'd_stay', status: 'closed', voted: 4, tally: [3, 1], result_index: 0, closed_at: '2026-10-05T12:00:00Z' }),
        reason: 'time',
      }, T),
    );
    await packs.apply(
      ev(9, 'mandate.set', {
        decision_id: 'd_stay',
        mandate: { choice_index: 0, cap_minor: 5_500_000, currency: 'INR', refundable_only: true, payer_person_id: 'p_rahul', set_at: '2026-10-05T12:01:00Z' },
      }, T),
    );
    await packs.apply(ev(10, 'mandate.confirmed', { decision_id: 'd_stay', person_id: 'p_rahul' }, T));
    await packs.apply(
      ev(11, 'record.changed', {
        line: {
          line_id: 'l_1', kind: 'agreed', text: 'North Goa it is', status: 'active', proof: [{ message_id: 'm_2' }],
          person_ids: ['p_priya'], source: 'go', created_at: '2026-10-05T12:02:00Z', updated_at: '2026-10-05T12:02:00Z',
        },
        change: 'added',
        by,
      }, T),
    );
    await packs.apply(ev(12, 'reaction.removed', { message_id: 'm_2', reaction: { emoji: '👍', person_id: 'p_rahul' } }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(13, 'message.edited', { message: msg(2, 'p_priya', 'scuba on sunday?', { updated_seq: 13, edited_at: '2026-10-05T12:03:00Z' }) }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(14, 'person.left', { person_id: 'p_zoya' }, T));
    pack = await packs.get(T);
    expect(pack.trip.decisions[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'closed', result_index: 0 });
    expect(pack.trip.decisions[0]?.mandate?.confirmed_at).toBeTruthy();
    expect(pack.trip.record.map((l) => l.line_id)).toEqual(['l_1']);
    expect(pack.messages[0]).toMatchObject({ text: 'scuba on sunday?', reactions: [] });
    expect(pack.trip.people.map((p) => p.person_id)).not.toContain('p_zoya');
    expect(pack.seq).toBe(14);

    await packs.apply(ev(15, 'message.deleted', { message_id: 'm_2' }, T));
    pack = await packs.get(T);
    expect(pack.messages).toEqual([]);
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query('select text, deleted_at, payload from messages where trip_id = $1 and message_id = $2', [T, 'm_2']);
    expect(rows[0].text).toBeNull();
    expect(rows[0].deleted_at).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(rows[0].payload.text).toBeUndefined();

    await packs.apply(ev(16, 'trip.updated', { by: { kind: 'person', person_id: 'p_rahul' }, changed: ['title'] }, T));
    expect((await packs.get(T)).trip.title).toBe('Goa trip');
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'read_trip')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('refill reads the trip again and returns only the messages Go never stored, page by page', async () => {
    const T = 't_gap';
    const { packs, calls } = make({
      read_trip: () => goaSnapshot({ trip_id: T, seq: 43 }),
      read_messages: (p) =>
        p.after_seq === 41
          ? { messages: [msg(42, 'p_arjun', '@Go are you there?')], has_more: true }
          : { messages: [msg(10, 'p_priya', 'old one', { updated_seq: 43 })], has_more: false },
    });
    await packs.apply(ev(1, 'trip.created', { trip: goaSnapshot({ trip_id: T }) }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(10, 'message.created', { message: msg(10, 'p_priya', 'old one') }, T));
    await packs.apply(ev(41, 'message.created', { message: msg(41, 'p_rahul', 'hmm') }, T));
    const fresh = await packs.refill(T, 41);
    expect(fresh.map((m) => m.seq)).toEqual([42]);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'read_messages').map((c) => c.params.after_seq)).toEqual([41, 42]);
    const pack = await packs.get(T);
    expect(pack.trip.seq).toBe(43);
    expect(pack.messages.map((m) => m.seq)).toEqual([10, 41, 42]);
  });

  it('invalidate drops a pack so the next get reads it fresh', async () => {
    const T = 't_drop';
    const { packs, calls } = make({ read_trip: () => goaSnapshot({ trip_id: T }), read_messages: () => ({ messages: [], has_more: false }) });
    await packs.get(T);
    packs.invalidate(T);
    await packs.get(T);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'read_trip')).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { Message } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { TripPack } from '../trips/pack.js';

/** Lane B (P2) owns this file; P1 ships the all-unsure stub so the rest of Go can be built first. */
export type Sure<T> = { value: T; sure: true } | { value: T | null; sure: false };

export interface ReflexRead {
  forGo: Sure<boolean>;
  complete: Sure<boolean>;
  size: Sure<'quick' | 'big'>;
  taskId: Sure<string | null>;
  fact: Sure<boolean>;
  recordKind: Sure<'agreed' | 'open' | 'doing' | null>;
  reaction: Sure<string | null>;
  risky: Sure<boolean>;
  opening: Sure<boolean>;
}

export interface Reflex {
  read(msg: Message, ctx: { pack: TripPack; runningTasks: Array<{ id: string; brief: string }> }): Promise<ReflexRead>;
}

const unsure = { value: null, sure: false } as const;

/** All nine answers unsure: the composer decides everything, exactly as if Jev were not there. */
export const UNSURE_READ: ReflexRead = {
  forGo: unsure,
  complete: unsure,
  size: unsure,
  taskId: unsure,
  fact: unsure,
  recordKind: unsure,
  reaction: unsure,
  risky: unsure,
  opening: unsure,
};

export const unsureReflex: Reflex = {
  read: async () => UNSURE_READ,
};
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/log.ts`:
```ts
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { TripEvent } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';

export interface TripLog {
  append(ev: TripEvent): Promise<'new' | 'duplicate'>;
  lastSeq(tripId: string): Promise<number>;
  /** Lanes react to trip changes (memory mirror, tasks, money). Called in change-number order per trip. */
  onEvent(fn: (ev: TripEvent) => void | Promise<void>): void;
}

export type TripLogImpl = TripLog & { emit(ev: TripEvent): Promise<void> };

export function createTripLog(o: { pool: pg.Pool; log: Log; now: () => Date }): TripLogImpl {
  const listeners: Array<(ev: TripEvent) => void | Promise<void>> = [];
  return {
    async append(ev) {
      const c = await o.pool.connect();
      try {
        await c.query('begin');
        const r = await c.query(
          `insert into trip_events (event_id, trip_id, seq, type, payload, received_at)
           values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::jsonb, $6) on conflict (event_id) do nothing`,
          [ev.event_id, ev.trip_id, ev.seq, ev.type, JSON.stringify(ev), o.now()],
        );
        if (r.rowCount === 1) {
          await c.query(
            `insert into trips (trip_id, last_seq, active_at) values ($1, $2, $3)
             on conflict (trip_id) do update set last_seq = greatest(trips.last_seq, excluded.last_seq), active_at = excluded.active_at`,
            [ev.trip_id, ev.seq, o.now()],
          );
        }
        await c.query('commit');
        return r.rowCount === 1 ? 'new' : 'duplicate';
      } catch (e) {
        await c.query('rollback');
        throw e;
      } finally {
        c.release();
      }
    },
    async lastSeq(tripId) {
      const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ last_seq: number }>('select last_seq from trips where trip_id = $1', [tripId]);
      return rows[0]?.last_seq ?? 0;
    },
    onEvent(fn) {
      listeners.push(fn);
    },
    async emit(ev) {
      for (const fn of listeners) {
        try {
          await fn(ev);
        } catch (e) {
          o.log.error({ err: e, event: ev.event_id, type: ev.type }, 'a trip log listener failed');
        }
      }
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/apply.ts`:
```ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { Message, TripEvent } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { TripPack } from './pack.js';

type Reaction = Message['reactions'][number];
const sameReaction = (a: Reaction, b: Reaction): boolean => a.emoji === b.emoji && (a.person_id ?? null) === (b.person_id ?? null);

function upsertBy<T>(list: T[], same: (x: T) => boolean, item: T): void {
  const i = list.findIndex(same);
  if (i >= 0) list[i] = item;
  else list.push(item);
}

/** Puts a message in the pack in seq order; a deleted message leaves it. */
export function upsertMessage(pack: TripPack, m: Message): void {
  const i = pack.messages.findIndex((x) => x.message_id === m.message_id);
  if (m.deleted) {
    if (i >= 0) pack.messages.splice(i, 1);
    return;
  }
  if (i >= 0) {
    pack.messages[i] = m;
    return;
  }
  pack.messages.push(m);
  const n = pack.messages.length;
  if (n > 1 && (pack.messages[n - 2]?.seq ?? 0) > m.seq) pack.messages.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
}

/**
 * Applies one event to a pack. 'reread' means the event says something changed without the new
 * values (trip.updated, bookings, expenses, or a vote Go has not seen), so the caller reads the trip.
 */
export function applyEvent(pack: TripPack, ev: TripEvent): 'ok' | 'reread' {
  const t = pack.trip;
  const decision = (id: string): C.Decision | undefined => t.decisions.find((d) => d.decision_id === id);
  switch (ev.type) {
    case 'trip.created':
      pack.trip = ev.data.trip;
      break;
    case 'message.created':
    case 'message.edited':
      upsertMessage(pack, ev.data.message);
      break;
    case 'message.deleted': {
      const id = ev.data.message_id;
      pack.messages = pack.messages.filter((m) => m.message_id !== id);
      break;
    }
    case 'reaction.added': {
      const data = ev.data;
      const m = pack.messages.find((x) => x.message_id === data.message_id);
      if (m && !m.reactions.some((r) => sameReaction(r, data.reaction))) m.reactions.push(data.reaction);
      break;
    }
    case 'reaction.removed': {
      const data = ev.data;
      const m = pack.messages.find((x) => x.message_id === data.message_id);
      if (m) m.reactions = m.reactions.filter((r) => !sameReaction(r, data.reaction));
      break;
    }
    case 'person.joined': {
      const p = ev.data.person;
      t.people = [...t.people.filter((x) => x.person_id !== p.person_id), p];
      break;
    }
    case 'person.updated': {
      const p = ev.data.person;
      t.people = t.people.map((x) => (x.person_id === p.person_id ? p : x));
      break;
    }
    case 'person.left': {
      const id = ev.data.person_id;
      t.people = t.people.filter((x) => x.person_id !== id);
      break;
    }
    case 'decision.opened':
    case 'decision.closed': {
      const d = ev.data.decision;
      upsertBy(t.decisions, (x) => x.decision_id === d.decision_id, d);
      break;
    }
    case 'decision.voted': {
      const data = ev.data;
      const d = decision(data.decision_id);
      if (!d) return 'reread';
      d.voted = data.voted;
      d.of = data.of;
      d.tally = data.tally;
      const pid = data.person_id;
      const idx = data.option_index;
      if (!d.anonymous && pid != null && idx != null) {
        d.votes = [...(d.votes ?? []).filter((v) => v.person_id !== pid), { person_id: pid, option_index: idx }];
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'decision.cancelled': {
      const d = decision(ev.data.decision_id);
      if (!d) return 'reread';
      d.status = 'cancelled';
      break;
    }
    case 'mandate.set': {
      const d = decision(ev.data.decision_id);
      if (!d) return 'reread';
      d.mandate = ev.data.mandate;
      break;
    }
    case 'mandate.confirmed':
    case 'mandate.declined': {
      const d = decision(ev.data.decision_id);
      if (!d?.mandate) return 'reread';
      if (ev.type === 'mandate.confirmed') {
        d.mandate.confirmed_at = ev.occurred_at;
        d.mandate.declined_at = null;
      } else {
        d.mandate.declined_at = ev.occurred_at;
        d.mandate.confirmed_at = null;
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'record.changed': {
      const line = ev.data.line;
      upsertBy(t.record, (x) => x.line_id === line.line_id, line);
      break;
    }
    case 'trip.updated':
    case 'booking.changed':
    case 'expense.changed':
      return 'reread';
  }
  pack.seq = Math.max(pack.seq, ev.seq);
  pack.trip.seq = Math.max(pack.trip.seq, ev.seq);
  return 'ok';
}
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/pack.ts`:
```ts
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { Message, Trip, TripEvent } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';
import { applyEvent, upsertMessage } from './apply.js';

/** An active trip's working set, kept fresh from events. */
export interface TripPack {
  /** TripSnapshot as read_trip returns it, kept current from events. */
  trip: Trip;
  /** The whole chat, oldest first, deletes removed. */
  messages: Message[];
  seq: number;
}

export interface Packs {
  get(tripId: string): Promise<TripPack>;
  invalidate(tripId: string): void;
}

export type PacksImpl = Packs & {
  /** Keeps a loaded pack current; stores every message whether or not the pack is loaded. */
  apply(ev: TripEvent): Promise<void>;
  /** read_trip plus read_messages after `after` (default: the newest change Go stored); returns messages Go never stored. */
  refill(tripId: string, after?: number): Promise<Message[]>;
  /** Trips whose pack was used within `withinMs` (default 6 hours). */
  active(withinMs?: number): string[];
  peek(tripId: string): TripPack | undefined;
};

/** Stores a message (newest change wins). True when Go had never stored it before. */
async function storeMessage(pool: pg.Pool, tripId: string, m: Message, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
  const payload = m.deleted ? { ...m, text: null } : m;
  const r = await pool.query<{ inserted: boolean }>(
    `insert into messages (trip_id, message_id, seq, updated_seq, person_id, text, reply_to, deleted_at, payload)
     values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9::jsonb)
     on conflict (trip_id, message_id) do update
       set updated_seq = excluded.updated_seq, text = excluded.text, deleted_at = excluded.deleted_at, payload = excluded.payload
       where messages.updated_seq <= excluded.updated_seq
     returning (xmax = 0) as inserted`,
    [
      tripId,
      m.message_id,
      m.seq,
      m.updated_seq,
      m.author.kind === 'person' ? m.author.person_id : null,
      m.deleted ? null : (m.text ?? null),
      m.reply_to ?? null,
      m.deleted ? now : null,
      JSON.stringify(payload),
    ],
  );
  return r.rows[0]?.inserted === true;
}

export function createPacks(o: { pool: pg.Pool; link: ServerLink; log: Log; now: () => Date }): PacksImpl {
  const packs = new Map<string, TripPack>();
  const touched = new Map<string, number>();
  const loading = new Map<string, Promise<TripPack>>();
  const touch = (tripId: string): void => {
    touched.set(tripId, o.now().getTime());
  };

  async function storedUpTo(tripId: string): Promise<number> {
    const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ n: number }>('select coalesce(max(updated_seq), 0)::int as n from messages where trip_id = $1', [tripId]);
    return rows[0]?.n ?? 0;
  }

  /** Reads messages changed after `after`, page by page while has_more; stores them; returns the ones Go never stored. */
  async function pull(tripId: string, after: number, pack: TripPack): Promise<Message[]> {
    const fresh: Message[] = [];
    let cursor = after;
    for (let page = 0; page < 50; page++) {
      const r = await o.link.request('read_messages', { trip_id: tripId, after_seq: cursor, limit: 200 });
      for (const m of r.messages) {
        if (await storeMessage(o.pool, tripId, m, o.now())) fresh.push(m);
        upsertMessage(pack, m);
        cursor = Math.max(cursor, m.updated_seq);
      }
      if (!r.has_more || r.messages.length === 0) break;
    }
    return fresh;
  }

  async function load(tripId: string): Promise<TripPack> {
    const trip = await o.link.request('read_trip', { trip_id: tripId });
    const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ payload: Message }>(
      'select payload from messages where trip_id = $1 and deleted_at is null order by seq',
      [tripId],
    );
    const pack: TripPack = { trip, messages: rows.map((r) => r.payload), seq: trip.seq };
    await pull(tripId, await storedUpTo(tripId), pack);
    packs.set(tripId, pack);
    return pack;
  }

  async function get(tripId: string): Promise<TripPack> {
    touch(tripId);
    const have = packs.get(tripId);
    if (have) return have;
    let p = loading.get(tripId);
    if (!p) {
      p = load(tripId).finally(() => loading.delete(tripId));
      loading.set(tripId, p);
    }
    return p;
  }

  return {
    get,
    invalidate(tripId) {
      packs.delete(tripId);
    },
    peek: (tripId) => packs.get(tripId),
    async apply(ev) {
      touch(ev.trip_id);
      if (ev.type === 'message.created' || ev.type === 'message.edited') {
        await storeMessage(o.pool, ev.trip_id, ev.data.message, o.now());
      }
      if (ev.type === 'message.deleted') {
        await o.pool.query(
          `update messages set deleted_at = $3, text = null, payload = (payload - 'text') || '{"deleted": true}'::jsonb
           where trip_id = $1 and message_id = $2`,
          [ev.trip_id, ev.data.message_id, o.now()],
        );
      }
      if (ev.type === 'trip.created' && !packs.has(ev.trip_id)) {
        packs.set(ev.trip_id, { trip: ev.data.trip, messages: [], seq: ev.seq });
        return;
      }
      const pack = packs.get(ev.trip_id);
      if (!pack) return; // not loaded: the next get() reads it fresh
      if (applyEvent(pack, ev) === 'reread') pack.trip = await o.link.request('read_trip', { trip_id: ev.trip_id });
      pack.seq = Math.max(pack.seq, ev.seq);
    },
    async refill(tripId, after) {
      const pack = await get(tripId);
      pack.trip = await o.link.request('read_trip', { trip_id: tripId });
      pack.seq = Math.max(pack.seq, pack.trip.seq);
      return pull(tripId, after ?? (await storedUpTo(tripId)), pack);
    },
    active(withinMs = 6 * 60 * 60_000) {
      const since = o.now().getTime() - withinMs;
      return [...touched].filter(([id, at]) => at >= since && packs.has(id)).map(([id]) => id);
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import type { Message } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';
import type { ReflexRead } from '../reflex/reflex.js';
import type { TripPack } from './pack.js';

export interface MessageHooks {
  /** Runs after the door has answered, in seq order per trip. P2 saves facts and quiet ✅ reactions; P6 looks for openings. */
  onRead(fn: (msg: Message, read: ReflexRead, pack: TripPack) => Promise<void>): void;
}

export type HooksImpl = MessageHooks & { run(msg: Message, read: ReflexRead, pack: TripPack): Promise<void> };

export function createMessageHooks(log: Log): HooksImpl {
  const fns: Array<(msg: Message, read: ReflexRead, pack: TripPack) => Promise<void>> = [];
  return {
    onRead(fn) {
      fns.push(fn);
    },
    async run(msg, read, pack) {
      for (const fn of fns) {
        try {
          await fn(msg, read, pack);
        } catch (e) {
          log.error({ err: e, message: msg.message_id }, 'a message hook failed');
        }
      }
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/composer/__tests__/sanitize.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { clip, sanitize, sanitizeDeep } from '../sanitize.js';

describe('sanitize', () => {
  it('turns em and en dashes into commas, and number ranges into hyphens', () => {
    expect(sanitize('Villa Sol — great pick')).toBe('Villa Sol, great pick');
    expect(sanitize('Villa Sol—great pick')).toBe('Villa Sol, great pick');
    expect(sanitize('Flights 6–9 pm')).toBe('Flights 6-9 pm');
    expect(sanitize('— hi')).toBe('hi');
    expect(sanitize('ok —.')).toBe('ok.');
  });

  it('never names the model or who made it', () => {
    expect(sanitize("Sure — I'm Claude, made by Anthropic.")).toBe("Sure, I'm Go, made by the Go team.");
    expect(sanitize('Running on Sonnet 5.5 today')).toBe('Running on Go today');
  });

  it('leaves plain text alone', () => {
    const t = 'Flights from Delhi after 6 pm on Friday: three options, from ₹5,400.';
    expect(sanitize(t)).toBe(t);
  });

  it('cleans every text field of a card, never an id, a link or a time', () => {
    const card = {
      kind: 'notice', card_id: 'c_—1', fallback_text: 'Heads up — check-in is at 2', tone: 'info',
      title: 'Check-in — 2 pm', body: 'Villa Sol — early check-in is possible',
      source: { name: 'Villa Sol', url: 'https://example.com/a—b' }, checked_at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z',
    };
    const out = sanitizeDeep(card);
    expect(out.fallback_text).toBe('Heads up, check-in is at 2');
    expect(out.title).toBe('Check-in, 2 pm');
    expect(out.body).toBe('Villa Sol, early check-in is possible');
    expect(out.card_id).toBe('c_—1');
    expect(out.source.url).toBe('https://example.com/a—b');
  });

  it('clips long text to the limit', () => {
    const t = clip('x'.repeat(2_100), 2_000);
    expect(t).toHaveLength(2_000);
    expect(t.endsWith('...')).toBe(true);
    expect(clip('short', 2_000)).toBe('short');
  });
});
```

```ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type pg from 'pg';
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { fakeLink } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/fake-link.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { createSpeak } from '../speak.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

const notice = (id: string): C.Card => ({ kind: 'notice', card_id: id, fallback_text: `Note ${id}`, tone: 'info', title: 'Heads up', body: 'Check-in is at 2 pm.' });

describe('typing', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('goes on at once, repeats every 5 s while a turn works, and goes off once', () => {
    const { link, notes } = fakeLink();
    const speak = createSpeak({ pool: {} as pg.Pool, link, now: () => new Date() });
    speak.typingOn('t_goa');
    expect(notes).toEqual([{ method: 'typing', params: { trip_id: 't_goa', on: true } }]);
    speak.working('t_goa');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(12_000);
    expect(notes.filter((n) => n.params.on)).toHaveLength(3);
    speak.typingOff('t_goa');
    speak.typingOff('t_goa');
    expect(notes.at(-1)).toEqual({ method: 'typing', params: { trip_id: 't_goa', on: false } });
    expect(notes.filter((n) => !n.params.on)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('stays honest: goes off by itself when no turn keeps it alive', () => {
    const { link, notes } = fakeLink();
    const speak = createSpeak({ pool: {} as pg.Pool, link, now: () => new Date() });
    speak.typingOn('t_goa');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(15_000);
    expect(notes.at(-1)?.params.on).toBe(false);
  });

  it('working() turns typing on when it was off, and says when it first went on', () => {
    const { link, notes } = fakeLink();
    const speak = createSpeak({ pool: {} as pg.Pool, link, now: () => new Date('2026-10-06T09:00:00Z') });
    const since = speak.working('t_goa');
    expect(since.toISOString()).toBe('2026-10-06T09:00:00.000Z');
    expect(notes).toEqual([{ method: 'typing', params: { trip_id: 't_goa', on: true } }]);
    speak.stopAll();
    expect(notes.at(-1)?.params.on).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('post', () => {
  it('sends the text with the first card and the other cards after, each with a stable request id, cleaned', async () => {
    const { link, calls } = fakeLink({ post_message: () => ({ message_id: `m_go_${calls.length}`, seq: 10 + calls.length }) });
    await db.pool.query(`insert into turns (turn_id, trip_id, woke_for, started_at, model) values ('turn_A', 't_goa', '{message}', now(), 'm')`);
    const speak = createSpeak({ pool: db.pool, link, now: () => new Date() });
    let n = 0;
    const ids = await speak.post({
      link,
      tripId: 't_goa',
      turnId: 'turn_A',
      requestId: (tag) => `go_${tag}_turn_A_${++n}`,
      text: 'Here you go — two picks',
      cards: [notice('c_1'), notice('c_2')],
      replyTo: 'm_4',
    });
    expect(ids).toEqual(['m_go_1', 'm_go_2']);
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.params.request_id)).toEqual(['go_post_turn_A_1', 'go_post_turn_A_2']);
    expect(calls[0]?.params).toMatchObject({ trip_id: 't_goa', text: 'Here you go, two picks', card: { card_id: 'c_1' }, reply_to: 'm_4' });
    expect(calls[1]?.params.text).toBeUndefined();
    expect(calls[1]?.params.card.card_id).toBe('c_2');
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query(`select posted_at from turns where turn_id = 'turn_A'`);
    expect(rows[0].posted_at).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
  });

  it('posts a card with no text, and nothing when there is nothing to post', async () => {
    const { link, calls } = fakeLink({ post_message: () => ({ message_id: 'm_go_9', seq: 9 }) });
    const speak = createSpeak({ pool: db.pool, link, now: () => new Date() });
    const base = { link, tripId: 't_goa', turnId: 'turn_B', requestId: (tag: string) => `go_${tag}_turn_B_1` };
    expect(await speak.post({ ...base, text: null, cards: [] })).toEqual([]);
    expect(await speak.post({ ...base, text: '   ', cards: [] })).toEqual([]);
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await speak.post({ ...base, text: null, cards: [notice('c_9')] })).toEqual(['m_go_9']);
    expect(calls[0]?.params.text).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

`apps/brain/src/composer/sanitize.ts`:
```ts
const RANGE = /(\d)\s*[–—]\s*(\d)/g;
const DASH = /\s*[—–]\s*/g;

/**
 * What every outgoing string passes before a person sees it: no em or en dashes (a dash between
 * words becomes a comma, and a number range written with a dash becomes a hyphen), and never the model's
 * or its maker's name.
 */
export function sanitize(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(RANGE, '$1-$2')
    .replace(DASH, ', ')
    .replace(/^[,\s]+/, '')
    .replace(/,\s*([,.!?])/g, '$1')
    .replace(/\bclaude\b/gi, 'Go')
    .replace(/\banthropic\b/gi, 'the Go team')
    .replace(/\b(?:sonnet|haiku|opus|fable)[\s-]*\d+(?:[.-]\d+)?\b/gi, 'Go')
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
    .trim();
}

const KEEP = /(_id|_ids|url|_at)$/;

/** sanitize() on every string inside a card, except ids, links and times. */
export function sanitizeDeep<T>(value: T, key = ''): T {
  if (typeof value === 'string') return (KEEP.test(key) ? value : sanitize(value)) as T;
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((v) => sanitizeDeep(v, key)) as T;
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([k, v]) => [k, sanitizeDeep(v, k)])) as T;
  }
  return value;
}

export function clip(text: string, max: number): string {
  return text.length <= max ? text : `${text.slice(0, max - 3).trimEnd()}...`;
}
```

`apps/brain/src/composer/speak.ts`:
```ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import { clip, sanitize, sanitizeDeep } from './sanitize.js';

export interface PostArgs {
  link: ServerLink;
  tripId: string;
  turnId: string;
  /** A stable request id per write ('post' is the tag): the same on a rerun of the same turn. */
  requestId: (tag: string) => string;
  text: string | null;
  cards: C.Card[];
  replyTo?: string;
  mentions?: string[];
}

export interface Speak {
  /** "Go is typing..." now, for a message that plainly names Go. Goes off by itself after 12 s unless a turn keeps it. */
  typingOn(tripId: string): void;
  /** A turn is working: typing on (if it was not) and kept alive until typingOff. Returns when it first went on. */
  working(tripId: string): Date;
  typingOff(tripId: string): void;
  /** Posts the text with the first card, then each other card; returns the message ids. */
  post(p: PostArgs): Promise<string[]>;
  stopAll(): void;
}

interface TypingState {
  since: Date;
  until: number;
  timer?: NodeJS.Timeout;
}

export function createSpeak(o: {
  pool: pg.Pool;
  link: ServerLink;
  now: () => Date;
  everyMs?: number;
  idleKeepMs?: number;
  workKeepMs?: number;
}): Speak {
  const everyMs = o.everyMs ?? 5_000;
  const idleKeepMs = o.idleKeepMs ?? 12_000;
  const workKeepMs = o.workKeepMs ?? 25_000;
  const typing = new Map<string, TypingState>();
  const send = (tripId: string, on: boolean): void => o.link.notify('typing', { trip_id: tripId, on });

  function typingOff(tripId: string): void {
    const st = typing.get(tripId);
    if (!st) return;
    clearInterval(st.timer);
    typing.delete(tripId);
    send(tripId, false);
  }

  // "On" lasts 10 s on the Server, so it is sent again every 5 s while it should stay on.
  function keep(tripId: string, keepMs: number): Date {
    const cur = typing.get(tripId);
    if (cur) {
      cur.until = Math.max(cur.until, Date.now() + keepMs);
      return cur.since;
    }
    send(tripId, true);
    const st: TypingState = { since: o.now(), until: Date.now() + keepMs };
    st.timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (Date.now() >= st.until) typingOff(tripId);
      else send(tripId, true);
    }, everyMs);
    // Never keeps the process alive; buildBrain stops every interval through brain.onStop (speak.stopAll).
    st.timer.unref();
    typing.set(tripId, st);
    return st.since;
  }

  return {
    typingOn(tripId) {
      keep(tripId, idleKeepMs);
    },
    working(tripId) {
      return keep(tripId, workKeepMs);
    },
    typingOff,
    async post(p) {
      const text = p.text && p.text.trim() ? clip(sanitize(p.text), 2_000) : null;
      const cards = p.cards.map((c) => sanitizeDeep(c));
      const first = cards.shift();
      const ids: string[] = [];
      if (text || first) {
        const r = await p.link.request('post_message', {
          request_id: p.requestId('post'),
          trip_id: p.tripId,
          ...(text ? { text } : {}),
          ...(first ? { card: first } : {}),
          ...(p.replyTo ? { reply_to: p.replyTo } : {}),
          ...(p.mentions?.length ? { mentions: p.mentions } : {}),
        });
        ids.push(r.message_id);
      }
      for (const card of cards) {
        const r = await p.link.request('post_message', { request_id: p.requestId('post'), trip_id: p.tripId, card });
        ids.push(r.message_id);
      }
      if (ids.length) {
        await o.pool.query('update turns set posted_at = coalesce(posted_at, $2) where turn_id = $1', [p.turnId, o.now()]);
      }
      return ids;
    },
    stopAll() {
      for (const tripId of [...typing.keys()]) typingOff(tripId);
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';

import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import { createCardStore, type CardStore } from '../cards.js';
import { createTapRouter } from '../taps.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
let cards: CardStore;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
  cards = createCardStore({ pool: db.pool, now: () => new Date() });
  await cards.record('t_goa', working('c_work'), 'm_go_1');
  await cards.record('t_goa', { kind: 'notice', card_id: 'c_note', fallback_text: 'Note', tone: 'info', title: 'Heads up', body: 'Check-in is at 2 pm.' }, 'm_go_2');
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

function working(id: string): C.Card {
  return {
    kind: 'working', card_id: id, fallback_text: 'Looking for villas', task_id: 'task_1', title: 'Villas in North Goa',
    state: 'running', steps: [{ text: 'Searching', state: 'doing' }], updated_at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00Z',
    actions: [{ action_id: 'watch', label: 'Watch', style: 'primary' }],
  };
}

let n = 0;
const tap = (over: Record<string, string> = {}) => ({
  tap_id: `tap_${++n}`, trip_id: 't_goa', card_id: 'c_work', action_id: 'watch', person_id: 'p_priya', ...over,
});
const meta = { instance: 'server-1', frameId: 'gs_1' };

describe('the tap router', () => {
  it('routes a tap by the card kind and the action, with the stored card', async () => {
    const r = router();

      seen.push(`${t.person_id}:${card.kind}`);
      return { result: 'open', url: 'https://go.example/watch/abc', expires_at: '2026-10-05T10:05:00Z' };
    });
    expect(await r.handle(tap(), meta)).toEqual({ result: 'open', url: 'https://go.example/watch/abc', expires_at: '2026-10-05T10:05:00Z' });
    expect(seen).toEqual(['p_priya:working']);
  });

  it('acts once per tap_id: a repeat gets the first answer, at the same moment or after a restart', async () => {
    const r = router();
    let runs = 0;
    r.on('working', 'stop', async () => {
      runs += 1;
      return { result: 'done', toast: 'Stopped' };
    });
    const t = tap({ action_id: 'stop', tap_id: 'tap_once' });
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([r.handle(t, meta), r.handle(t, meta)]);
    const c = await r.handle(t, meta);
    expect(runs).toBe(1);
    expect(a).toEqual({ result: 'done', toast: 'Stopped' });
    expect(b).toEqual(a);
    expect(c).toEqual(a);
    const afterRestart = router();

  });

  it('refuses kindly a card Go does not know, or a button with no handler', async () => {
    const r = router();
    expect(await r.handle(tap({ card_id: 'c_gone' }), meta)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That card is not active any more.' });
    expect(await r.handle(tap({ action_id: 'zoom' }), meta)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: "That button doesn't do anything yet." });
  });

    r.on('notice', 'slow', async () => {

    });
    r.on('notice', 'broken', async () => {
      throw new Error('boom');

    expect(await r.handle(tap({ card_id: 'c_note', action_id: 'slow' }), meta)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That took too long. Try again in a moment.' });
    expect(await r.handle(tap({ card_id: 'c_note', action_id: 'broken' }), meta)).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'Something went wrong. Try again in a moment.' });

    expect(await r.handle(t, meta)).toEqual(first);
    expect(await r.dispatch(t)).toEqual(first);

    expect(runs).toBe(1);
    expect(await r.dispatch(tap({ card_id: 'c_gone', tap_id: 'tap_admin_2' }))).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That card is not active any more.' });

    r.on('options', 'pick', async () => ({ result: 'done' }));
    expect(() => r.on('options', 'pick', async () => ({ result: 'done' }))).toThrow(/already/);

});

describe('the card store', () => {

    expect(rows[0].message_id).toBe('m_go_7');
    expect(await cards.get('t_goa', 'c_missing')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`apps/brain/src/door/cards.ts`:
```ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { ToServerMethod } from './methods.js';

/** Go's room cards, so a tap can be routed by the card's kind. Never payment card details. */
export interface CardStore {
  record(tripId: string, card: C.Card, messageId: string | null): Promise<void>;
  get(tripId: string, cardId: string): Promise<C.Card | null>;
  /** ServerLink's onResult: keeps every card Go posts or replaces. */
  observe(method: ToServerMethod, params: Record<string, unknown>, result: unknown): Promise<void>;
}

export function createCardStore(o: { pool: pg.Pool; now: () => Date }): CardStore {
  async function record(tripId: string, card: C.Card, messageId: string | null): Promise<void> {
    await o.pool.query(
      `insert into cards (trip_id, card_id, kind, message_id, card, updated_at) values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::jsonb, $6)
       on conflict (trip_id, card_id) do update
         set card = excluded.card, message_id = coalesce(excluded.message_id, cards.message_id), updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
      [tripId, card.card_id, card.kind, messageId, JSON.stringify(card), o.now()],
    );
  }
  return {
    record,
    async get(tripId, cardId) {
      const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ card: C.Card }>('select card from cards where trip_id = $1 and card_id = $2', [tripId, cardId]);
      return rows[0]?.card ?? null;
    },
    async observe(method, params, result) {
      if (method !== 'post_message' && method !== 'replace_card') return;
      const card = params.card as C.Card | null | undefined;
      const tripId = params.trip_id;
      if (!card || typeof tripId !== 'string') return;
      const messageId = (result as { message_id?: unknown } | null)?.message_id;
      await record(tripId, card, typeof messageId === 'string' ? messageId : null);
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/door/taps.ts`:
```ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type pg from 'pg';
import { sanitize } from '../composer/sanitize.js';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';
import type { CardStore } from './cards.js';
import type { ToGoHandler } from './handlers.js';
import type { CardTapParams, CardTapResult } from './methods.js';

export type GoCardKind = 'options' | 'working' | 'call' | 'pay' | 'notice';
const GO_CARD_KINDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set<GoCardKind>(['options', 'working', 'call', 'pay', 'notice']);

type TapHandler = (tap: CardTapParams, card: C.Card) => Promise<CardTapResult>;

export interface TapRouter {
  /** Go cards only: options, working, call, pay, notice. One answer per tap_id: a repeat gets the first answer back. */
  on(kind: GoCardKind, actionId: string, h: TapHandler): void;
  /** Runs a tap as if the Server sent it (the admin page's "Decline the waiting pay card"); same once-per-tap_id rule. */
  dispatch(tap: CardTapParams): Promise<CardTapResult>;
}

/** What the door registers for card_tap. `dispatch` and `handle` share one path, so a tap_id is acted on once either way. */
export type TapRouterImpl = TapRouter & { handle: ToGoHandler<'card_tap'> };

class TooSlow extends Error {}

const refused = (toast: string): CardTapResult => ({ result: 'refused', toast });

function clean(r: CardTapResult): CardTapResult {
  if (r.result === 'open') return r;
  const toast = r.toast ? sanitize(r.toast).slice(0, 120) : '';
  if (r.result === 'refused') return { result: 'refused', toast: toast || 'That did not work.' };
  return toast ? { result: 'done', toast } : { result: 'done' };
}

export function createTapRouter(o: { pool: pg.Pool; cards: CardStore; log: Log; limitMs?: number }): TapRouterImpl {
  // The Server waits about 3.5 s and never resends a tap, so the answer must be ready well inside 3 s.
  const limitMs = o.limitMs ?? 2_500;
  const routes = new Map<string, TapHandler>();
  const inflight = new Map<string, Promise<CardTapResult>>();

  async function stored(tapId: string): Promise<CardTapResult | null> {
    const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ result: CardTapResult }>('select result from card_taps where tap_id = $1', [tapId]);
    return rows[0]?.result ?? null;
  }

  async function run(h: TapHandler, tap: CardTapParams, card: C.Card): Promise<CardTapResult> {
    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
    try {
      return await Promise.race([
        h(tap, card),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
          timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new TooSlow()), limitMs);
        }),
      ]);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof TooSlow) return refused('That took too long. Try again in a moment.');
      o.log.error({ err: e, card: tap.card_id, action: tap.action_id }, 'a card tap failed');
      return refused('Something went wrong. Try again in a moment.');
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  /** One answer per tap_id, from the Server (handle) or from inside Go (dispatch). */
  function once(tap: CardTapParams): Promise<CardTapResult> {
    const cur = inflight.get(tap.tap_id);
    if (cur) return cur;
    const p = decide(tap).finally(() => inflight.delete(tap.tap_id));
    inflight.set(tap.tap_id, p);
    return p;
  }

  async function decide(tap: CardTapParams): Promise<CardTapResult> {
    const first = await stored(tap.tap_id);
    if (first) return first;
    const card = await o.cards.get(tap.trip_id, tap.card_id);
    const h = card ? routes.get(`${card.kind}:${tap.action_id}`) : undefined;
    const result = clean(
      !card ? refused('That card is not active any more.') : !h ? refused("That button doesn't do anything yet.") : await run(h, tap, card),
    );
    const ins = await o.pool.query<{ result: CardTapResult }>(
      `insert into card_taps (tap_id, trip_id, card_id, action_id, person_id, result) values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6::jsonb)
       on conflict (tap_id) do nothing returning result`,
      [tap.tap_id, tap.trip_id, tap.card_id, tap.action_id, tap.person_id, JSON.stringify(result)],
    );
    return ins.rows[0]?.result ?? (await stored(tap.tap_id)) ?? result;
  }

  return {
    on(kind, actionId, h) {
      if (!GO_CARD_KINDS.has(kind)) throw new Error(`${kind} is not a Go card: the Server owns its buttons`);
      const key = `${kind}:${actionId}`;
      if (routes.has(key)) throw new Error(`${key} already has a handler`);
      routes.set(key, h);
    },
    handle: (tap) => once(tap),
    dispatch: (tap) => once(tap),
  };
}
```
