`apps/brain/src/composer/__tests__/loop.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { fakeAnthropic, type Script } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/fake-anthropic.js';
import { fakeLink } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/fake-link.js';
import { goaSnapshot, msg } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import type { TextBlock, TurnLimits } from '../context.js';
import { countMarkers, MAX_ROUNDS, runLoop, type LoopInput } from '../loop.js';
import { roomTools } from '../room-tools.js';
import { makeTurnCtx, type QuickTool } from '../tools.js';

const HOUR = { type: 'ephemeral', ttl: '1h' } as const;
const SYSTEM: TextBlock[] = [{ type: 'text', text: 'You are Go.', cache_control: HOUR }];
const USER: TextBlock[] = [
  { type: 'text', text: '# The trip chat so far, oldest first' },
  { type: 'text', text: '<data source="chat, part 1">\n#2 Priya: hi\n</data>', cache_control: HOUR },
  { type: 'text', text: '# Right now\nIt is Tue, 6 Oct, 2:30 pm.' },
  { type: 'text', text: '# New since your last turn\n<data source="new messages">\n#3 Arjun: @Go hello\n</data>' },
];
const echo: QuickTool<{ say: string }> = {
  name: 'echo', description: 'Says it back.', input: z.object({ say: z.string() }), run: async (a) => ({ said: a.say }),
};
const echoCall = (say: string) => ({ name: 'echo', input: { say } });

function setup(script: Script, more: Partial<LoopInput> = {}, limits: TurnLimits | null = null) {
  const { client, bodies } = fakeAnthropic(script);
  const { link } = fakeLink({ add_reaction: () => ({ seq: 9 }) });
  const ctx = makeTurnCtx({
    tripId: 't_goa', turnId: 'turn_L', link, wake: [], signal: new AbortController().signal,
    pack: { trip: goaSnapshot(), messages: [msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go hello')], seq: 3 },
    limits,
  });
  const run = () =>
    runLoop({
      client, model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', system: SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: USER }],
      tools: [echo, ...roomTools({ now: () => new Date() })], ctx, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5_000), ...more,
    });
  return { bodies, ctx, run };
}
const markersIn = (body: any) => countMarkers(body.system, body.tools, body.messages);

describe('the composer loop', () => {
  it('one round: the final text is the answer, and the wire body is what Go needs', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup([{ text: 'Hello!' }]);
    const r = await run();
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ text: 'Hello!', silent: false, rounds: 1, stopReason: 'end_turn' });
    const body = bodies[0];
    expect(body.max_tokens).toBe(1024);
    expect(body.thinking).toEqual({ type: 'between_tools' });
    expect(body.tool_choice).toEqual({ type: 'auto' });
    expect(body.system).toEqual([{ type: 'text', text: 'You are Go.', cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral', ttl: '1h' } }]);
    expect(body.messages).toEqual([{ role: 'user', content: USER }]);
    expect(body.tools.map((t: any) => t.name)).toEqual(['echo', 'react', 'open_vote', 'close_vote', 'update_trip', 'stay_silent']);
    for (const t of body.tools) {
      expect(t.input_schema.type).toBe('object');
      expect(t.input_schema.$schema).toBeUndefined();
    }
    expect(markersIn(body)).toBe(2);
  });

  it('sends no thinking field on Haiku 4.5', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup([{ text: 'Hi' }], { model: 'claude-haiku-4-5' });
    await run();
    expect(bodies[0].thinking).toBeUndefined();
    expect(bodies[0].max_tokens).toBe(1024);
  });

  it('a tool round runs the calls together and puts one rolling marker on the newest block', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup([{ tools: [echoCall('a'), echoCall('b')] }, { text: 'Done.' }]);
    const r = await run();
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ text: 'Done.', rounds: 2 });
    const second = bodies[1];
    expect(second.messages).toHaveLength(3);
    const results = second.messages[2].content;
    expect(results.map((b: any) => b.content)).toEqual(['{"said":"a"}', '{"said":"b"}']);
    expect(results.at(-1).cache_control).toEqual({ type: 'ephemeral' });
    expect(results[0].cache_control).toBeUndefined();
    expect(second.messages[0].content[1].cache_control).toEqual({ type: 'ephemeral', ttl: '1h' });
    expect(markersIn(second)).toBe(3);
  });

  it('stay_silent ends the turn in the same round, with no extra call', async () => {
    const { bodies, ctx, run } = setup([
      { tools: [{ name: 'react', input: { message_id: '#3', emoji: '👍' } }, { name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'just a hello' } }] },
      { text: 'never asked' },
    ]);
    const r = await run();
    expect(bodies).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ text: '', silent: true, stopReason: 'stay_silent' });
    expect(ctx.outcome).toMatchObject({ silent: true, reactions: 1 });
  });

  it('stops after 4 rounds, and no request ever carries more than 4 markers', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup(() => ({ tools: [echoCall('again')] }));
    const r = await run();
    expect(bodies).toHaveLength(MAX_ROUNDS);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ rounds: 4, stopReason: 'max_rounds' });
    for (const b of bodies) expect(markersIn(b)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(4);
    expect(markersIn(bodies[3])).toBe(3);
  });

  it('gives bad arguments back to the model to fix', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup([{ tools: [{ name: 'react', input: { emoji: '👍' } }] }, { text: 'Fixed.' }]);
    await run();
    const result = bodies[1].messages[2].content[0];
    expect(result.is_error).toBe(true);
    expect(result.content).toContain('Invalid arguments for react');
  });

  it('an unknown tool is an error result, not a crash', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup([{ tools: [{ name: 'pay_now', input: {} }] }, { text: 'ok' }]);
    await run();
    expect(bodies[1].messages[2].content[0]).toMatchObject({ is_error: true, content: 'There is no tool named pay_now.' });
  });

  it('a turn with limits refuses any other tool and never runs it; stay_silent always works; the definitions never change', async () => {
    let echoed = 0;
    const counted: QuickTool<{ say: string }> = { ...echo, run: async (a) => { echoed += 1; return { said: a.say }; } };
    const free = setup([{ text: 'Hi' }]);
    await free.run();
    const limited = setup(
      [
        { tools: [echoCall('x'), { name: 'react', input: { message_id: '#3', emoji: '👍' } }] },
        { tools: [{ name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'noted' } }] },
      ],
      { tools: [counted, ...roomTools({ now: () => new Date() })] },
      { tools: ['react'], mayPostText: false },
    );
    const r = await limited.run();
    expect(echoed).toBe(0);
    const results = limited.bodies[1].messages[2].content;
    expect(results[0]).toMatchObject({ is_error: true });
    expect(results[0].content).toContain('echo is not allowed this turn');
    expect(results[1].is_error).toBeUndefined();
    expect(limited.ctx.outcome.reactions).toBe(1);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ silent: true, stopReason: 'stay_silent' });
    // The same tool definitions as a turn with no limits, so the one-hour cache on the rules and tools still reads.
    expect(limited.bodies[0].tools).toEqual(free.bodies[0].tools);
    expect(limited.bodies[0].system).toEqual(free.bodies[0].system);
  });

  it('when the last round writes nothing, the latest earlier text is the answer', async () => {
    const { run } = setup([{ text: 'Opening a vote now!', tools: [echoCall('x')] }, {}]);
    expect((await run()).text).toBe('Opening a vote now!');
  });

  it('sums the usage of every round, with the cost', async () => {
    const { run } = setup([
      { tools: [echoCall('x')], usage: { input_tokens: 50, cache_read_input_tokens: 4_000, output_tokens: 10 } },
      { text: 'ok', usage: { input_tokens: 60, cache_read_input_tokens: 4_100, cache_creation_input_tokens: 200, output_tokens: 20 } },
    ]);
    const { usage } = await run();
    expect(usage).toMatchObject({ input: 110, cacheRead: 8_100, cacheWrite: 200, output: 30 });
    expect(usage.costUsd).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('the turn\'s signal stops a slow model', async () => {
    const { run } = setup([{ text: 'late', delayMs: 2_000 }], { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(50) });
    await expect(run()).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('calls the first-token callback once', async () => {
    let n = 0;
    const { run } = setup([{ tools: [echoCall('x')] }, { text: 'two' }], { onFirstToken: () => { n += 1; } });
    await run();
    expect(n).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a continued exchange carries the rolling marker from its first round', async () => {
    const { bodies, run } = setup([{ text: 'ok' }], { continuing: true });
    await run();
    expect(bodies[0].messages[0].content.at(-1).cache_control).toEqual({ type: 'ephemeral' });
    expect(markersIn(bodies[0])).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

`apps/brain/src/composer/anthropic.ts`:
```ts
import Anthropic from '@anthropic-ai/sdk';

export interface StreamLike extends AsyncIterable<Anthropic.MessageStreamEvent> {
  finalMessage(): Promise<Anthropic.Message>;
}

/**
 * The part of the Anthropic client Go uses: the composer streams; the lanes' side passes (P2's notes and fold) make
 * one plain call. buildBrain hands the same client to every lane as brain.anthropic, so tests and the bench inject a fake.
 */
export interface AnthropicLike {
  messages: {
    stream(body: Anthropic.MessageStreamParams, options?: { signal?: AbortSignal }): StreamLike;
    create(body: Anthropic.MessageCreateParamsNonStreaming, options?: { signal?: AbortSignal }): Promise<Anthropic.Message>;
  };
}

/** The real client. maxRetries 0: the desk retries a failed turn; the loop never waits on its own. */
export function anthropicClient(apiKey: string): AnthropicLike {
  return new Anthropic({ apiKey, maxRetries: 0 }) as unknown as AnthropicLike;
}
```

`apps/brain/src/composer/loop.ts`:
```ts
import type Anthropic from '@anthropic-ai/sdk';
import { computeAnthropicCostUsd, resolveCapabilities, type AnthropicUsage } from '@vestra/agent-sdk';
import { normalizeAnthropicUsage } from '@vestra/agent-sdk/engine';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { AnthropicLike } from './anthropic.js';
import type { TextBlock } from './context.js';
import { STAY_SILENT, type QuickTool, type TurnCtx } from './tools.js';

export const MAX_ROUNDS = 4;
export const MAX_TOKENS = 1_024;
const ROLLING = { type: 'ephemeral' } as const; // 5 minutes: it only has to last the turn

export interface Usage {
  /** Input not read from or written to the cache. */
  input: number;
  cacheRead: number;
  cacheWrite: number;
  output: number;
  costUsd: number;
}
export const noUsage = (): Usage => ({ input: 0, cacheRead: 0, cacheWrite: 0, output: 0, costUsd: 0 });
export function addUsage(into: Usage, u: Usage): void {
  into.input += u.input;
  into.cacheRead += u.cacheRead;
  into.cacheWrite += u.cacheWrite;
  into.output += u.output;
  into.costUsd += u.costUsd;
}

export interface LoopInput {
  client: AnthropicLike;
  model: string;
  system: TextBlock[];
  messages: Anthropic.MessageParam[];
  tools: QuickTool<any>[];
  ctx: TurnCtx;
  signal: AbortSignal;
  maxRounds?: number;
  /** True when this call continues an exchange (the pre-post check): its first round already carries the rolling marker. */
  continuing?: boolean;
  onFirstToken?: () => void;
  toolTimeoutMs?: number;
}

export interface LoopResult {
  /** What to post: the last round's text, or the latest text an earlier round wrote. Empty when silent. */
  text: string;
  silent: boolean;
  rounds: number;
  usage: Usage;
  /** The whole exchange so far, ending with the model's last message or the last tool results. */
  messages: Anthropic.MessageParam[];
  stopReason: string | null;
}

/** Tool definitions from the zod inputs. */
export function toolDefs(tools: QuickTool<any>[]): Anthropic.Tool[] {
  return tools.map((t) => {
    const schema = z.toJSONSchema(t.input, { target: 'draft-2020-12', unrepresentable: 'any' }) as Record<string, unknown>;
    delete schema.$schema;
    return { name: t.name, description: t.description, input_schema: schema as Anthropic.Tool['input_schema'] };
  });
}

type Marked = { cache_control?: { type: 'ephemeral'; ttl?: '5m' | '1h' } | null };
const isRolling = (b: Anthropic.ContentBlockParam): boolean => {
  const cc = (b as Marked).cache_control;
  return cc != null && cc.ttl !== '1h';
};
function unmark(b: Anthropic.ContentBlockParam): Anthropic.ContentBlockParam {
  const copy = { ...b } as Record<string, unknown>;
  delete copy.cache_control;
  return copy as unknown as Anthropic.ContentBlockParam;
}

/** Keeps the chat's one-hour marker and moves the one rolling marker to the newest block. */
export function withRollingMarker(messages: Anthropic.MessageParam[]): Anthropic.MessageParam[] {
  const out = messages.map((m) =>
    typeof m.content === 'string' ? m : { ...m, content: m.content.map((b) => (isRolling(b) ? unmark(b) : b)) },
  );
  const last = out[out.length - 1];
  if (!last || typeof last.content === 'string') return out;
  const tail = last.content[last.content.length - 1];
  if (!tail || (tail.type !== 'text' && tail.type !== 'tool_result')) return out;
  out[out.length - 1] = { ...last, content: [...last.content.slice(0, -1), { ...tail, cache_control: ROLLING } as Anthropic.ContentBlockParam] };
  return out;
}

/** Cache markers in a request; the API allows 4. */
export function countMarkers(system: unknown, tools: unknown, messages: unknown): number {
  return (JSON.stringify([system, tools, messages]).match(/"cache_control":\{/g) ?? []).length;
}

/** The assistant's content as request blocks: text, tool calls and any thinking, verbatim. */
function toParams(content: Anthropic.ContentBlock[]): Anthropic.ContentBlockParam[] {
  const out: Anthropic.ContentBlockParam[] = [];
  for (const b of content) {
    if (b.type === 'text' && b.text) out.push({ type: 'text', text: b.text });
    else if (b.type === 'tool_use') out.push({ type: 'tool_use', id: b.id, name: b.name, input: b.input });
    else if (b.type === 'thinking') out.push({ type: 'thinking', thinking: b.thinking, signature: b.signature });
    else if (b.type === 'redacted_thinking') out.push({ type: 'redacted_thinking', data: b.data });
  }
  return out;
}

function usageOf(u: Anthropic.Usage, model: string): Usage {
  const wire: AnthropicUsage = {
    input_tokens: u.input_tokens,
    output_tokens: u.output_tokens,
    cache_read_input_tokens: u.cache_read_input_tokens ?? 0,
    cache_creation_input_tokens: u.cache_creation_input_tokens ?? 0,
    ...(u.cache_creation
      ? {
          cache_creation: {
            ephemeral_5m_input_tokens: u.cache_creation.ephemeral_5m_input_tokens,
            ephemeral_1h_input_tokens: u.cache_creation.ephemeral_1h_input_tokens,
          },
        }
      : {}),
  };
  const n = normalizeAnthropicUsage(wire);
  return {
    input: n.inputTokens,
    cacheRead: n.cacheReadInputTokens ?? 0,
    cacheWrite: n.cacheCreationInputTokens ?? 0,
    output: n.outputTokens,
    costUsd: computeAnthropicCostUsd(wire, model),
  };
}

function withTimeout<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`the tool took longer than ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    const onAbort = (): void => reject(new Error('the turn ran out of time'));
    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    p.then(resolve, reject).finally(() => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
    });
  });
}

async function runTool(tool: QuickTool<any> | undefined, call: Anthropic.ToolUseBlock, i: LoopInput): Promise<Anthropic.ToolResultBlockParam> {
  const result = (content: string, isError = false): Anthropic.ToolResultBlockParam => ({
    type: 'tool_result',
    tool_use_id: call.id,
    content,
    ...(isError ? { is_error: true } : {}),
  });
  if (!tool) return result(`There is no tool named ${call.name}.`, true);
  // A turn with limits (P6's proactive bar): any other tool is refused before it runs. The definitions sent never change.
  const allowed = i.ctx.allowedTools;
  if (allowed && call.name !== STAY_SILENT && !allowed.includes(call.name)) {
    const can = [...new Set([...allowed, STAY_SILENT])].join(', ');
    return result(`${call.name} is not allowed this turn. You can use: ${can}.`, true);
  }
  const args = tool.input.safeParse(call.input);
  if (!args.success) return result(`Invalid arguments for ${call.name}: ${z.prettifyError(args.error)}. Fix them and call it again.`, true);
  try {
    const out = await withTimeout(tool.run(args.data, i.ctx), i.toolTimeoutMs ?? 8_000, i.signal);
    return result(typeof out === 'string' ? out : JSON.stringify(out ?? { ok: true }));
  } catch (e) {
    return result((e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)).slice(0, 500), true);
  }
}

/**
 * One composer turn's model rounds: a direct Messages tool loop. At most 4 rounds; the tools of a round
 * run together; stay_silent ends the turn with no extra call; every round's usage is summed.
 */
export async function runLoop(i: LoopInput): Promise<LoopResult> {
  const maxRounds = Math.min(i.maxRounds ?? MAX_ROUNDS, MAX_ROUNDS);
  const tools = toolDefs(i.tools);
  const byName = new Map(i.tools.map((t) => [t.name, t]));
  const caps = resolveCapabilities(i.model);
  // Thinking off: 'between_tools' on Sonnet 5.5 (which cannot omit it); no thinking field on other models.
  const thinking = caps.id === 'claude-sonnet-5-5' ? { type: 'between_tools' } : undefined;
  const messages = [...i.messages];
  const usage = noUsage();
  let firstToken = false;
  let latestText = '';
  for (let round = 1; round <= maxRounds; round++) {
    let outgoing = round > 1 || i.continuing ? withRollingMarker(messages) : messages;
    if (countMarkers(i.system, tools, outgoing) > 4) outgoing = messages;
    const body = {
      model: i.model,
      max_tokens: Math.min(MAX_TOKENS, caps.maxOutputTokens),
      system: i.system,
      tools,
      tool_choice: { type: 'auto' },
      messages: outgoing,
      ...(thinking ? { thinking } : {}),
    };
    // 'between_tools' is not in the SDK's types yet, so the body is cast.
    const stream = i.client.messages.stream(body as unknown as Anthropic.MessageStreamParams, { signal: i.signal });
    for await (const ev of stream) {
      if (!firstToken && ev.type === 'content_block_delta') {
        firstToken = true;
        i.onFirstToken?.();
      }
    }
    const reply = await stream.finalMessage();
    addUsage(usage, usageOf(reply.usage, i.model));
    messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: toParams(reply.content) });
    const text = reply.content
      .filter((b): b is Anthropic.TextBlock => b.type === 'text')
      .map((b) => b.text)
      .join('')
      .trim();
    if (text) latestText = text;
    const calls = reply.content.filter((b): b is Anthropic.ToolUseBlock => b.type === 'tool_use');
    if (reply.stop_reason !== 'tool_use' || calls.length === 0) {
      return { text: text || latestText, silent: i.ctx.outcome.silent, rounds: round, usage, messages, stopReason: reply.stop_reason };
    }
    const results = await Promise.all(calls.map((c) => runTool(byName.get(c.name), c, i)));
    messages.push({ role: 'user', content: results });
    if (calls.some((c) => c.name === STAY_SILENT)) {
      i.ctx.outcome.silent = true;
      return { text: '', silent: true, rounds: round, usage, messages, stopReason: STAY_SILENT };
    }
  }
  return { text: latestText, silent: i.ctx.outcome.silent, rounds: maxRounds, usage, messages, stopReason: 'max_rounds' };
}
```

### Task 13: The composer turn: one turn per desk wake, the pre-post check, giving up calmly

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/composer/turn.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/composer/__tests__/turn.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TurnInput`, `TurnRunner`, `DeskItem` (Task 9); `ServerLink`, `createServerLink` (Task 4); `Packs`, `TripPack` (Task 6); `ContextImpl` (with `turnRules`), `TurnLimits` (Task 10); `chatLine`, `dataBlock`, `personName` (Task 10); `runLoop`, `addUsage`, `noUsage`, `MAX_ROUNDS` (Task 12); `AnthropicLike` (Task 12); `Speak` (Task 7); `Tools`, `makeTurnCtx`, `scopedLink` (Task 11); `classifyAnthropicError` from `@vestra/agent-sdk/engine`.
- Produces: `ComposerDeps`, `Composer { runTurn: TurnRunner; giveUp: TurnRunner }`, `createComposer(d): Composer`, `TROUBLE`. Each turn asks `context.turnRules(items)` and puts the answer on its `TurnCtx`: the loop refuses tools outside `allowedTools`, and when `mayPostText` is false the final text is not posted (and the pre-post check is skipped, so new messages wait for their own turn) while the cards in `ctx.cards` still go.

`apps/brain/src/composer/__tests__/turn.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fakeAnthropic, type Script } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/fake-anthropic.js';
import { goaSnapshot, msg } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import type { DeskItem } from '../../desk/desk.js';
import type { Message } from '../../door/methods.js';
import { createServerLink } from '../../door/server-link.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import type { TripPack } from '../../trips/pack.js';
import { createContext, type TurnLimits } from '../context.js';
import { roomTools } from '../room-tools.js';
import { createSpeak } from '../speak.js';
import { createTools } from '../tools.js';
import { createComposer, TROUBLE } from '../turn.js';
import { renderMessageWake, renderSetupWake, renderTripChangeWake } from '../wakes.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

let rooms = 0;
/** A composer on a real ServerLink (so go_requests is real) whose socket answers like the Server. */
function room(o: {
  messages: Message[];
  script: Script;
  claimNew?: (tripId: string, turnId: string) => Promise<DeskItem[]>;
  limits?: (wake: DeskItem[]) => TurnLimits | null;
}) {
  const tripId = `t_turn_${++rooms}`;
  const calls: Array<{ method: string; params: any }> = [];
  const notes: Array<{ method: string; params: any }> = [];
  const link = createServerLink({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, timeoutMs: 1_000 });
  const answers: Record<string, () => Record<string, unknown>> = {
    post_message: () => ({ message_id: `m_go_${calls.length}`, seq: 100 + calls.length }),
    add_reaction: () => ({ seq: 100 + calls.length }),
    open_vote: () => ({ decision_id: 'd_first', seq: 100 + calls.length }),
  };
  link.attach({
    id: 's1',
    instance: 'test',
    send: (frame) => {
      const f = JSON.parse(frame);
      if (f.id === undefined) {
        notes.push({ method: f.method, params: f.params });
        return;
      }
      calls.push({ method: f.method, params: f.params });
      const answer = answers[f.method];
      setImmediate(() =>
        link.onAnswer(answer ? { kind: 'result', id: f.id, result: answer() } : { kind: 'error', id: f.id, error: { code: -32601, message: 'not here' } }),
      );
    },
  });
  const pack: TripPack = { trip: goaSnapshot({ trip_id: tripId }), messages: [...o.messages], seq: 100 };
  const tools = createTools();
  for (const t of roomTools({ now: () => new Date() })) tools.register(t);
  const context = createContext(silentLog);
  context.addWake('message', renderMessageWake);
  context.addWake('trip_change', renderTripChangeWake);
  context.addWake('setup', renderSetupWake);
  if (o.limits) context.addTurnRules(o.limits);
  const model = fakeAnthropic(o.script);
  const composer = createComposer({
    pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date(), link,
    packs: { get: async () => pack, invalidate: () => undefined },
    tools, context, speak: createSpeak({ pool: db.pool, link, now: () => new Date() }),
    client: model.client, model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', claimNew: o.claimNew ?? (async () => []),
  });
  return { tripId, calls, notes, model, composer, pack, posts: () => calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'post_message') };
}

const item = (tripId: string, m: Message, namesGo: boolean): DeskItem => ({
  id: `msg:${tripId}:${m.message_id}`, tripId, kind: 'message', ref: m.message_id, dueAt: new Date(), payload: { messageId: m.message_id, seq: m.seq, namesGo },
});

async function turn(r: ReturnType<typeof room>, items: DeskItem[], turnId = `turn_${r.tripId}`, attempt = 1) {
  await db.pool.query('insert into trips (trip_id) values ($1) on conflict do nothing', [r.tripId]);
  await r.composer.runTurn({ tripId: r.tripId, turnId, items, attempt });
}

describe('a composer turn', () => {
  it('"@Go ..." gets one post in the model\'s words, cleaned, and the turn is written down', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go flights Delhi to Goa Friday evening?');
    const r = room({ messages: [msg(2, 'p_priya', 'Goa in November!'), ask], script: [{ text: 'Friday evening — three flights after 6 pm, checking fares now.' }] });
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)]);
    expect(r.posts()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params).toEqual({
      request_id: `go_post_turn_${r.tripId}_1`, trip_id: r.tripId, text: 'Friday evening, three flights after 6 pm, checking fares now.',
    });
    expect(r.notes.map((x) => x.params.on)).toEqual([true, false]);
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query('select * from turns where turn_id = $1', [`turn_${r.tripId}`]);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'spoke', rounds: 1, input_tokens: 100, output_tokens: 20, woke_for: ['message'] });
    for (const k of ['typing_at', 'first_token_at', 'posted_at', 'ended_at']) expect(rows[0][k]).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    const seen = await db.pool.query('select composer_seen_seq from trips where trip_id = $1', [r.tripId]);
    expect(seen.rows[0].composer_seen_seq).toBe(3);
    expect(r.model.bodies[0].messages[0].content.at(-1).text).toContain('#3 Arjun: @Go flights Delhi to Goa Friday evening?');
  });

  it('chatter: the model stays silent, nothing is posted and typing never shows', async () => {
    const lol = msg(4, 'p_meher', 'lol');
    const r = room({ messages: [lol], script: [{ tools: [{ name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'chatter' } }] }] });
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, lol, false)]);
    expect(r.posts()).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.notes).toEqual([]);
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query('select outcome from turns where turn_id = $1', [`turn_${r.tripId}`]);
    expect(rows[0].outcome).toBe('silent');
  });

  it('the greeting: the first vote opens and its card rides with hello', async () => {
    const r = room({
      messages: [],
      script: [
        { tools: [{ name: 'open_vote', input: { question: 'Where should we stay?', options: [{ label: 'North Goa villa' }, { label: 'South Goa resort' }] } }] },
        { text: 'Hi all, I am Go! I can find stays, run votes and keep the plan.' },
      ],
    });
    await turn(r, [{ id: `setup:${r.tripId}:greet`, tripId: r.tripId, kind: 'setup', ref: 'greet', dueAt: new Date(), payload: {} }]);
    expect(r.calls.map((c) => c.method)).toEqual(['open_vote', 'post_message']);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params).toMatchObject({
      text: 'Hi all, I am Go! I can find stays, run votes and keep the plan.',
      card: { kind: 'vote', decision_id: 'd_first', card_id: `c_vote_turn_${r.tripId}_1` },
    });
    expect(r.model.bodies[0].messages[0].content.at(-2).text).toContain('you just joined this trip');
    expect(r.notes).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('the pre-post check: a message that lands while Go writes joins the same turn', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go flights Friday?');
    const more = msg(5, 'p_kabir', 'also Saturday?');
    let claimed = false;
    const r = room({
      messages: [ask],
      script: [{ text: 'Friday: three flights after 6 pm.' }, { text: 'Friday has three flights after 6 pm, Saturday has two.' }],
      claimNew: async (tripId) => {
        if (claimed) return [];
        claimed = true;
        r.pack.messages.push(more);
        return [item(tripId, more, false)];
      },
    });
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)]);
    expect(r.model.bodies).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(r.model.bodies[0].messages[0].content.at(-1).text).not.toContain('#5 ');
    const fold = r.model.bodies[1].messages.at(-1).content[0].text;
    expect(fold).toContain('# New while you were writing');
    expect(fold).toContain('#5 Kabir: also Saturday?');
    expect(r.posts()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params.text).toBe('Friday has three flights after 6 pm, Saturday has two.');
    const seen = await db.pool.query('select composer_seen_seq from trips where trip_id = $1', [r.tripId]);
    expect(seen.rows[0].composer_seen_seq).toBe(5);
  });

  it('a rerun after the message went out posts nothing more and asks the model nothing', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go hi');
    const r = room({ messages: [ask], script: [{ text: 'Hey!' }] });
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)]);
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)], `turn_${r.tripId}`, 2);
    expect(r.posts()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.model.bodies).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('a crash before posting: the rerun repeats the same request ids, so nothing is done twice', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go is the villa pet friendly?');
    const react = { name: 'react', input: { message_id: '#3', emoji: '👀' } };
    const r = room({ messages: [ask], script: [{ tools: [react] }, { throws: new Error('overloaded') }, { tools: [react] }, { text: 'Yes, pets are welcome.' }] });
    await expect(turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)])).rejects.toThrow('overloaded');
    const failed = await db.pool.query('select outcome, error from turns where turn_id = $1', [`turn_${r.tripId}`]);
    expect(failed.rows[0].outcome).toBe('failed');
    expect(failed.rows[0].error).toContain('overloaded');
    expect(r.notes.at(-1)?.params.on).toBe(false);
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)], `turn_${r.tripId}`, 2);
    expect(r.calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'add_reaction')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.posts()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('giving up: one calm message, only when someone was waiting', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go hello?');
    const r = room({ messages: [ask], script: [] });
    const t = { tripId: r.tripId, turnId: `turn_${r.tripId}`, items: [item(r.tripId, ask, true)], attempt: 3 };
    await r.composer.giveUp(t);
    await r.composer.giveUp(t);
    expect(r.posts()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params).toMatchObject({ request_id: `go_trouble_turn_${r.tripId}`, text: TROUBLE });
    const quiet = room({ messages: [], script: [] });
    await quiet.composer.giveUp({
      tripId: quiet.tripId, turnId: 'turn_quiet', attempt: 3,
      items: [{ id: 'g', tripId: quiet.tripId, kind: 'setup', ref: 'greet', dueAt: new Date(), payload: {} }],
    });
    expect(quiet.posts()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('replies to the message that named Go when others spoke after it', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_arjun', '@Go what time is check-in?');
    const after = msg(4, 'p_meher', 'and checkout?');
    const r = room({ messages: [ask, after], script: [{ text: 'Check-in is at 2 pm.' }] });
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true), item(r.tripId, after, false)]);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params.reply_to).toBe('m_3');
  });

  it('a turn Go woke for on its own keeps its limits: no text when it may not post, but its card still goes', async () => {
    const r = room({
      messages: [msg(3, 'p_arjun', 'villa or resort, guys?')],
      limits: (wake) => (wake.every((w) => w.kind === 'opening') ? { tools: ['open_vote'], mayPostText: false } : null),
      script: [
        {
          tools: [
            { name: 'open_vote', input: { question: 'Villa or resort?', options: [{ label: 'Villa' }, { label: 'Resort' }] } },
            { name: 'update_trip', input: { stay: 'resort' } },
          ],
        },
        { text: 'Opened a vote for you all!' },
      ],
    });
    await turn(r, [{ id: `opening:${r.tripId}:stay`, tripId: r.tripId, kind: 'opening', ref: 'stay', dueAt: new Date(), payload: {} }]);
    expect(r.calls.map((c) => c.method)).toEqual(['open_vote', 'post_message']);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params.text).toBeUndefined();
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params.card).toMatchObject({ kind: 'vote', decision_id: 'd_first' });
    const refused = r.model.bodies[1].messages[2].content.find((b: { is_error?: boolean }) => b.is_error);
    expect(refused.content).toContain('update_trip is not allowed this turn');
    // The definitions sent are the full list, as on any other turn.
    expect(r.model.bodies[0].tools.map((t: { name: string }) => t.name)).toEqual(['close_vote', 'open_vote', 'react', 'stay_silent', 'update_trip']);
    const { rows } = await db.pool.query('select outcome from turns where turn_id = $1', [`turn_${r.tripId}`]);
    expect(rows[0].outcome).toBe('acted');
  });

  it('a tool outside the limits never reaches the Server, and the text still posts when it may', async () => {
    const ask = msg(3, 'p_priya', '@Go any news?');
    const r = room({
      messages: [ask],
      limits: () => ({ tools: ['react'], mayPostText: true }),
      script: [
        { tools: [{ name: 'open_vote', input: { question: 'When?', options: [{ label: 'Fri' }, { label: 'Sat' }] } }] },
        { text: 'Nothing new yet, I will say when the fares move.' },
      ],
    });
    await turn(r, [item(r.tripId, ask, true)]);
    expect(r.calls.map((c) => c.method)).toEqual(['post_message']);
    expect(r.posts()[0]?.params.text).toBe('Nothing new yet, I will say when the fares move.');
  });
});
```

`apps/brain/src/composer/turn.ts`:
```ts
import { classifyAnthropicError } from '@vestra/agent-sdk/engine';
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { DeskItem, TurnInput, TurnRunner } from '../desk/desk.js';
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';
import type { Packs, TripPack } from '../trips/pack.js';
import type { AnthropicLike } from './anthropic.js';
import type { ContextImpl } from './context.js';
import { chatLine, dataBlock, personName } from './fence.js';
import { addUsage, MAX_ROUNDS, noUsage, runLoop } from './loop.js';
import type { Speak } from './speak.js';
import { makeTurnCtx, scopedLink, type Tools } from './tools.js';

export interface ComposerDeps {
  pool: pg.Pool;
  log: Log;
  now: () => Date;
  link: ServerLink;
  packs: Packs;
  tools: Tools;
  context: ContextImpl;
  speak: Speak;
  client: AnthropicLike;
  model: string;
  /** The desk's claimNew: message items that arrived while this turn ran. */
  claimNew: (tripId: string, turnId: string) => Promise<DeskItem[]>;
  turnTimeoutMs?: number;
}

export interface Composer {
  runTurn: TurnRunner;
  giveUp: TurnRunner;
}

export const TROUBLE = "Sorry, I'm having trouble right now. Give me a minute and ask me again?";

const isForGo = (i: DeskItem): boolean => i.kind === 'message' && (i.payload as { namesGo?: boolean } | null)?.namesGo === true;
const messageIdOf = (i: DeskItem): string => (i.payload as { messageId?: string } | null)?.messageId ?? i.ref;

/** Reply to the one message that named Go when others spoke after it, so the thread stays clear. */
function replyToFor(items: DeskItem[], pack: TripPack): string | null {
  const named = items.filter(isForGo);
  const only = named.length === 1 ? named[0] : undefined;
  if (!only) return null;
  const id = messageIdOf(only);
  const m = pack.messages.find((x) => x.message_id === id);
  if (!m) return null;
  return pack.messages.some((x) => x.seq > m.seq && x.author.kind === 'person') ? id : null;
}

/** The chat lines of desk items' messages, as the context draws them. */
function linesFor(items: DeskItem[], pack: TripPack): { lines: string[]; seqs: number[] } {
  const names = new Map(pack.trip.people.map((p) => [p.person_id, personName(p.name)]));
  const seqs = new Map(pack.messages.map((m) => [m.message_id, m.seq]));
  const out = { lines: [] as string[], seqs: [] as number[] };
  for (const i of items) {
    const m = pack.messages.find((x) => x.message_id === messageIdOf(i));
    const line = m ? chatLine(m, (id) => names.get(id) ?? 'Someone who left', (id) => seqs.get(id)) : null;
    if (m && line) {
      out.lines.push(line);
      out.seqs.push(m.seq);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

const foldText = (lines: string[]): string =>
  `# New while you were writing\n${dataBlock('new messages', lines)}\n` +
  'Your reply above has not been posted yet. Write the one message to post now, covering these too if they need it. ' +
  'If they change nothing, send the same reply again.';

export function createComposer(d: ComposerDeps): Composer {
  const turnTimeoutMs = d.turnTimeoutMs ?? 20_000;

  async function alreadyPosted(turnId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const r = await d.pool.query(
      `select 1 from go_requests where turn_id = $1 and method = 'post_message' and answered_at is not null and error is null limit 1`,
      [turnId],
    );
    return (r.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
  }

  async function runTurn(t: TurnInput): Promise<void> {
    // A rerun of a turn whose message already went out: the room has its answer.
    if (await alreadyPosted(t.turnId)) return;
    const pack = await d.packs.get(t.tripId);
    const forGo = t.items.some(isForGo);
    // Typing stays honest while the turn works: on now (if it was not), off when the turn ends.
    const typingAt = forGo ? d.speak.working(t.tripId) : null;
    const times: { firstToken: Date | null } = { firstToken: null };
    const usage = noUsage();
    let rounds = 0;
    try {
      const trip = await d.pool.query<{ composer_seen_seq: number; tz: string }>('select composer_seen_seq, tz from trips where trip_id = $1', [t.tripId]);
      const seenSeq = trip.rows[0]?.composer_seen_seq ?? 0;
      const tz = trip.rows[0]?.tz ?? 'Asia/Kolkata';
      await d.pool.query(
        `insert into turns (turn_id, trip_id, woke_for, attempt, started_at, typing_at, model) values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)
         on conflict (turn_id) do update
           set attempt = excluded.attempt, started_at = excluded.started_at, typing_at = coalesce(turns.typing_at, excluded.typing_at), error = null`,
        [t.turnId, t.tripId, t.items.map((i) => i.kind), t.attempt, d.now(), typingAt, d.model],
      );
      const prompt = await d.context.build({ tripId: t.tripId, pack, wake: t.items, seenSeq, now: d.now(), tz });
      const signal = AbortSignal.timeout(turnTimeoutMs);
      // P6's proactive bar may limit a turn Go woke for on its own: which tools, and whether its text may be posted.
      const limits = d.context.turnRules(t.items);
      const ctx = makeTurnCtx({ tripId: t.tripId, turnId: t.turnId, link: d.link, pack, wake: t.items, signal, limits });
      const base = {
        client: d.client,
        model: d.model,
        system: prompt.system,
        tools: d.tools.list(),
        ctx,
        signal,
        onFirstToken: () => {
          times.firstToken ??= d.now();
        },
      };
      let r = await runLoop({ ...base, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt.user }] });
      addUsage(usage, r.usage);
      rounds += r.rounds;
      let newest = prompt.newestSeq;

      // Just before posting: messages that arrived meanwhile join this turn (one more round), instead of a stale answer.
      // A turn that may not post text skips it: those messages wait on the desk for their own turn.
      if (!r.silent && r.text && ctx.mayPostText !== false && rounds < MAX_ROUNDS) {
        const fresh = linesFor(await d.claimNew(t.tripId, t.turnId), pack);
        if (fresh.lines.length) {
          r = await runLoop({
            ...base,
            continuing: true,
            maxRounds: MAX_ROUNDS - rounds,
            messages: [...r.messages, { role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'text', text: foldText(fresh.lines) }] }],
          });
          addUsage(usage, r.usage);
          rounds += r.rounds;
          newest = Math.max(newest, ...fresh.seqs);
        }
      }

      // With mayPostText false the words are dropped; the cards in ctx.cards still go.
      const text = r.silent || ctx.mayPostText === false ? null : r.text.trim() || null;
      const replyTo = text ? replyToFor(t.items, pack) : null;
      const ids =
        text || ctx.cards.length
          ? await d.speak.post({
              link: ctx.link,
              tripId: t.tripId,
              turnId: t.turnId,
              requestId: (tag) => ctx.stableId(`go_${tag}`),
              text,
              cards: ctx.cards,
              ...(replyTo ? { replyTo } : {}),
            })
          : [];
      ctx.outcome.posts.push(...ids);
      ctx.outcome.spoke = text !== null && ids.length > 0;
      const outcome = ctx.outcome.spoke ? 'spoke' : ids.length > 0 || ctx.outcome.reactions > 0 ? 'acted' : 'silent';
      await d.pool.query(
        `update turns set ended_at = $2, first_token_at = $3, rounds = $4, input_tokens = $5, cached_tokens = $6,
           cache_write_tokens = $7, output_tokens = $8, cost_usd = $9, outcome = $10, error = null
         where turn_id = $1`,
        [t.turnId, d.now(), times.firstToken, rounds, usage.input, usage.cacheRead, usage.cacheWrite, usage.output, usage.costUsd, outcome],
      );
      await d.pool.query('update trips set composer_seen_seq = greatest(composer_seen_seq, $2) where trip_id = $1', [t.tripId, newest]);
    } catch (e) {
      const kind = classifyAnthropicError(e).kind;
      const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
      await d.pool
        .query(`update turns set ended_at = $2, outcome = 'failed', error = $3 where turn_id = $1`, [t.turnId, d.now(), `${kind}: ${message}`.slice(0, 500)])
        .catch(() => undefined);
      throw e;
    } finally {
      if (forGo) d.speak.typingOff(t.tripId);
    }
  }

  /** After the last failed attempt: one calm line, only if someone asked Go something and nothing went out yet. */
  async function giveUp(t: TurnInput): Promise<void> {
    if (!t.items.some(isForGo)) return;
    if (await alreadyPosted(t.turnId)) return;
    await d.speak.post({
      link: scopedLink(d.link, t.turnId),
      tripId: t.tripId,
      turnId: t.turnId,
      requestId: () => `go_trouble_${t.turnId}`,
      text: TROUBLE,
      cards: [],
    });
  }

  return { runTurn, giveUp };
}
```

### Task 14: Setup and intake: from send_event to the desk

**Before this task:** P2 Task 1 (`apps/brain/src/reflex/secrets.ts`, the secrets screen) is on `build`. It has no dependencies, and the P1 builder builds it right after Task 6, exactly as P2's plan writes it (file, test and commit), so this task can call it.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/trips/setup.ts`, `apps/brain/src/trips/names-go.ts`, `apps/brain/src/trips/screen.ts`, `apps/brain/src/trips/intake.ts`
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/trips/pack.ts` (Task 6: `pull` screens every message `read_messages` returns before it is stored or put in the pack)
- Test: `apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/setup.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/names-go.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/screen.test.ts`, `apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/intake.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TripLogImpl` (Task 6); `PacksImpl`, `TripPack` (Task 6); `HooksImpl` (Task 6); `Reflex`, `ReflexRead`, `UNSURE_READ` (Task 6); `Desk`, `createDesk` (Task 9); `Speak` (Task 7); `ToGoHandler` (Task 5); `Message`, `TripEvent` (Task 3); fixtures (Task 6). From P2 Task 1 (`reflex/secrets.ts`): `maskSecrets(input: string): { text: string; kinds: SecretKind[] }` (cards by Luhn, CVVs, one-time codes, PINs, passwords; `[card number hidden]` and `[hidden]`).
- Produces: master §6.3 `Setup { addStep; ensureTrip }` plus `SetupStep`, `SetupImpl = Setup & { ensureSoon(tripId); isReady(tripId); sweep(): Promise<number> }` and `createSetup(o)`; `namesGoInText(text)`, `plainlyNamesGo(m, pack)`; `screenMessage(m): Message`, `screenEvent(ev): TripEvent`; `IntakeDeps`, `createIntake(d)` returning `{ sendEvent: ToGoHandler<'send_event'>; recover(): Promise<number>; refreshActive(): Promise<void>; idle(): Promise<void> }`. Desk item ids P1 uses: `msg:<trip_id>:<message_id>`, `change:<event_id>`, `setup:<trip_id>:greet`.
- People's text is screened at the door (master §6.3 notes): `sendEvent` runs `screenEvent` before the event is written to `trip_events`, so the messages table, the pack, the reflex, the lanes' hooks and every prompt only ever see the masked text; messages read back with `read_messages` (cold load, a gap, the minute refresh) pass `screenMessage` in `pull` before they are stored. Go's own reads (`read_messages` has no `request_id`) are never kept in `go_requests`, so no copy of the raw text stays there either.

`apps/brain/src/trips/__tests__/setup.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import { createSetup } from '../setup.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

const steps = async (tripId: string) =>
  (await db.pool.query('select step, done_at, error from trip_setup where trip_id = $1 order by step', [tripId])).rows;

describe('setup', () => {
  it('runs the steps in order, after their prerequisites, and records when each was done', async () => {
    const s = createSetup({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
    const ran: string[] = [];
    s.addStep({ name: 'computer.warm', after: 'computer.user', run: async () => { ran.push('warm'); } });
    s.addStep({ name: 'brain.ready', run: async () => { ran.push('ready'); } });
    s.addStep({ name: 'computer.user', after: 'brain.ready', run: async () => { ran.push('user'); } });
    await s.ensureTrip('t_setup_1');
    expect(ran).toEqual(['ready', 'user', 'warm']);
    const rows = await steps('t_setup_1');
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.step)).toEqual(['brain.ready', 'computer.user', 'computer.warm']);
    expect(rows.every((r) => r.done_at instanceof Date)).toBe(true);
    expect(s.isReady('t_setup_1')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is safe to repeat: a done step never runs again, even after a restart', async () => {
    let runs = 0;
    const make = () => {
      const s = createSetup({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
      s.addStep({ name: 'brain.ready', run: async () => { runs += 1; } });
      return s;
    };
    const s = make();
    await s.ensureTrip('t_setup_2');
    await s.ensureTrip('t_setup_2');
    await make().ensureTrip('t_setup_2');
    expect(runs).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a failed step keeps its error, the steps after it wait, and the sweep finishes the job', async () => {
    let fail = true;
    let warmed = false;
    const s = createSetup({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
    s.addStep({ name: 'brain.ready', run: async () => undefined });
    s.addStep({ name: 'computer.user', after: 'brain.ready', run: async () => { if (fail) throw new Error('/v1 is down'); } });
    s.addStep({ name: 'computer.warm', after: 'computer.user', run: async () => { warmed = true; } });
    await s.ensureTrip('t_setup_3');
    expect(await steps('t_setup_3')).toMatchObject([
      { step: 'brain.ready', error: null },
      { step: 'computer.user', done_at: null, error: '/v1 is down' },
      { step: 'computer.warm', done_at: null, error: 'waits for computer.user' },
    ]);
    expect(s.isReady('t_setup_3')).toBe(false);
    fail = false;
    expect(await s.sweep()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect((await steps('t_setup_3')).every((r) => r.done_at instanceof Date)).toBe(true);
    expect(warmed).toBe(true);
    expect(s.isReady('t_setup_3')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('two calls at once share one run', async () => {
    let runs = 0;
    const s = createSetup({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date() });
    s.addStep({ name: 'brain.ready', run: async () => { runs += 1; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50)); } });
    await Promise.all([s.ensureTrip('t_setup_4'), s.ensureTrip('t_setup_4')]);
    expect(runs).toBe(1);
  });

  it('ensureSoon tries a trip that is not ready at most once every 30 s', async () => {
    let t = Date.parse('2026-10-06T09:00:00Z');
    let runs = 0;
    const s = createSetup({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now: () => new Date(t) });
    s.addStep({ name: 'brain.ready', run: async () => { runs += 1; throw new Error('not yet'); } });
    s.ensureSoon('t_setup_5');
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(runs).toBe(1));
    s.ensureSoon('t_setup_5');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
    expect(runs).toBe(1);
    t += 31_000;
    s.ensureSoon('t_setup_5');
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(runs).toBe(2));
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { goaSnapshot, msg } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import { plainlyNamesGo } from '../names-go.js';

const pack = { trip: goaSnapshot(), messages: [msg(2, null, 'Want a vote on the villa?')], seq: 2 };

describe('plainlyNamesGo: when "Go is typing..." shows at once', () => {
  it.each([
    ['@Go flights Friday?', true],
    ['Go, find us a villa', true],
    ['Go bhai abhi book kar de', true],
    ['thoughts, Go?', true],
    ['hey go what is the weather', true],
    ['thanks Go', true],
    ["let's go to the beach", false],
    ['go to sleep guys', false],
    ['goa is going to be hot', false],
    ['we should go', false],
    ['lol', false],
  ])('%s', (text, want) => {

  });

  it('a reply to Go names Go, and so does the Server\'s mentions_go', () => {
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ev, msg } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import { screenEvent, screenMessage } from '../screen.js';

describe('screen: card numbers, CVVs, codes and passwords never get past the door', () => {
  it('hides them in the text, photo captions and file names, and changes nothing else', () => {
    const m = msg(5, 'p_meher', 'OTP hai 482913', {
      attachments: [
        { kind: 'photo', url: 'https://cdn.go.example/p.jpg', caption: 'my card 4111-1111-1111-1111' },
        { kind: 'file', url: 'https://cdn.go.example/f.pdf', name: 'password is goa@123.pdf', mime: 'application/pdf', size_bytes: 1_200 },
        { kind: 'photo', url: 'https://cdn.go.example/q.jpg' },
      ],
    });
    const s = screenMessage(m);
    expect(s.text).toBe('OTP hai [hidden]');
    expect(s.attachments[0]).toMatchObject({ caption: 'my card [card number hidden]' });
    expect(s.attachments[1]).toMatchObject({ name: 'password is [hidden]' });
    expect(s.attachments[2]).toEqual(m.attachments[2]);
    expect({ ...s, text: m.text, attachments: m.attachments }).toEqual(m);
    expect(m.text).toBe('OTP hai 482913');
  });

  it('a deleted message with no text and a plain message come back as they were', () => {
    const gone = msg(6, 'p_rahul', 'x', { deleted: true, text: null });
    expect(screenMessage(gone)).toEqual(gone);
    const plain = msg(7, 'p_priya', 'flight 6E 2134 at 18:40, ₹54,200 per head');
    expect(screenMessage(plain)).toEqual(plain);
  });

  it('screens message.created and message.edited, and passes every other event through', () => {
    const created = ev(8, 'message.created', { message: msg(8, 'p_kabir', 'cvv 123 hai') });
    expect((screenEvent(created) as typeof created).data.message.text).toBe('cvv [hidden] hai');
    const edited = ev(9, 'message.edited', { message: msg(8, 'p_kabir', 'pin is 4321', { updated_seq: 9 }) });
    expect((screenEvent(edited) as typeof edited).data.message.text).toBe('pin is [hidden]');
    const left = ev(10, 'person.left', { person_id: 'p_kabir' });
    expect(screenEvent(left)).toBe(left);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { fakeLink, type FakeAnswers } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/fake-link.js';
import { ev, goaSnapshot, msg, vote } from '../../__tests__/fixtures/goa.js';
import type { Speak } from '../../composer/speak.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { createDesk } from '../../desk/desk.js';
import type { TripEvent } from '../../door/methods.js';
import { silentLog } from '../../log.js';
import { UNSURE_READ, type Reflex, type ReflexRead } from '../../reflex/reflex.js';
import { createMessageHooks } from '../hooks.js';
import { createIntake } from '../intake.js';
import { createTripLog } from '../log.js';
import { createPacks } from '../pack.js';
import { createSetup } from '../setup.js';

let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
});
afterAll(() => db.drop());

const sure = <T>(value: T) => ({ value, sure: true as const });
const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

function harness(o: { reflex?: (order: string[]) => Reflex; answers?: FakeAnswers } = {}) {
  const now = () => new Date();
  const order: string[] = [];
  const f = fakeLink({
    read_trip: (p) => goaSnapshot({ trip_id: p.trip_id, seq: 1 }),
    read_messages: () => ({ messages: [], has_more: false }),
    ...o.answers,
  });
  const tripLog = createTripLog({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now });
  const packs = createPacks({ pool: db.pool, link: f.link, log: silentLog, now });
  const setup = createSetup({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now });
  const desk = createDesk({ pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now, holder: 'intake-test', inProcessWake: false });
  const hooks = createMessageHooks(silentLog);
  hooks.onRead(async (m) => { order.push(`hook:${m.message_id}`); });
  const emitted: string[] = [];
  tripLog.onEvent((e) => { emitted.push(e.event_id); });
  const speak: Speak = {
    typingOn: (tripId) => { order.push(`typing:${tripId}`); },
    working: () => new Date(),
    typingOff: () => undefined,
    post: async () => [],
    stopAll: () => undefined,
  };
  // The same step registerRoom adds (Task 15).
  setup.addStep({
    name: 'brain.ready',
    run: async (tripId) => {
      const pack = await packs.get(tripId);
      if (!pack.messages.some((m) => m.author.kind === 'go')) {
        await desk.put({ id: `setup:${tripId}:greet`, tripId, kind: 'setup', ref: 'greet', dueAt: now(), payload: {} });
      }
    },
  });
  const reflex: Reflex = o.reflex?.(order) ?? { read: async () => { order.push('reflex'); return UNSURE_READ; } };
  const intake = createIntake({
    pool: db.pool, log: silentLog, now, tripLog, packs, setup, desk, speak, hooks,
    reflex: () => reflex, runningTasks: async () => [], waitMs: 2_000,
  });
  const send = (e: TripEvent) => intake.sendEvent(e, { instance: 'test', frameId: `gs_${e.event_id}` });
  return { intake, send, f, order, emitted, packs };
}

const open = async (tripId: string) =>
  (await db.pool.query(`select id, kind, ref, due_at, payload, done_at, error from desk_items where trip_id = $1 order by created_at, id`, [tripId])).rows;
const created = (tripId: string) => ev(1, 'trip.created', { trip: goaSnapshot({ trip_id: tripId }) }, tripId);

describe('intake', () => {
  it('answers send_event once the event is written; a repeat is a duplicate and is processed once', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const e = created('t_in_1');
    expect(await h.send(e)).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    expect(await h.send(e)).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: true });
    await h.intake.idle();
    expect(h.emitted).toEqual([e.event_id]);
  });

  it('trip.created: the pack comes from the event, setup runs, and Go is due to say hello', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    await h.send(created('t_in_2'));
    await h.intake.idle();
    await vi.waitFor(async () => expect((await open('t_in_2')).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(['setup:t_in_2:greet']));
    expect(h.f.calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'read_trip')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('"@Go ..." turns typing on at once, before the reflex answers, and waits 2 s on the desk', async () => {
    const h = harness({ reflex: (order) => ({ read: async () => { await sleep(100); order.push('reflex'); return UNSURE_READ; } }) });
    const T = 't_in_3';

    const item = (await open(T)).find((r) => r.kind === 'message');
    expect(item).toMatchObject({ id: `msg:${T}:m_2`, ref: 'm_2', payload: { messageId: 'm_2', seq: 2, namesGo: true } });

    await h.send(ev(4, 'message.created', { message: msg(4, 'p_priya', 'what time is check-in?') }, T));

    const items = (await open(T)).filter((r) => r.kind === 'message');
    expect(items.map((r) => r.ref)).toEqual(['m_3', 'm_4']);
    for (const r of items) expect(r.due_at.getTime()).toBeLessThanOrEqual(sentAt + 200);

  it('Go\'s own messages and withheld ones never wake Go', async () => {
    const h = harness();

  it('handles a trip\'s events one at a time in seq order, even when the reflex is slow', async () => {
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/setup.ts`:
```ts
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';

export interface SetupStep {
  name: string;
  /** Runs only once this step is done. */
  after?: string;
  run(tripId: string): Promise<void>;
}

/** Level-triggered setup: every step is safe to repeat, and its done time is kept in trip_setup. */
export interface Setup {
  /** P1 adds 'brain.ready'; P4 adds 'computer.user' and 'computer.warm'. */
  addStep(step: SetupStep): void;
  /** Runs every step not done yet, in order. Safe to repeat. */
  ensureTrip(tripId: string): Promise<void>;
}

export type SetupImpl = Setup & {
  /** ensureTrip in the background, for a trip not known to be ready; at most once every 30 s per trip. */
  ensureSoon(tripId: string): void;
  isReady(tripId: string): boolean;
  /** Finishes setups that stopped halfway; runs every 2 minutes. Returns how many trips it tried. */
  sweep(): Promise<number>;
};

export function createSetup(o: { pool: pg.Pool; log: Log; now: () => Date; retryMs?: number }): SetupImpl {
  const retryMs = o.retryMs ?? 30_000;
  const steps: SetupStep[] = [];
  const ready = new Set<string>();
  const lastTry = new Map<string, number>();
  const inflight = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();

  /** Steps in an order where each comes after the one it waits for. */
  function ordered(): SetupStep[] {
    const names = new Set(steps.map((s) => s.name));
    const placed = new Set<string>();
    const out: SetupStep[] = [];
    let moved = true;
    while (moved) {
      moved = false;
      for (const s of steps) {
        if (placed.has(s.name)) continue;
        if (!s.after || placed.has(s.after) || !names.has(s.after)) {
          out.push(s);
          placed.add(s.name);
          moved = true;
        }
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  async function mark(tripId: string, step: string, error: string | null): Promise<void> {
    await o.pool.query(
      `insert into trip_setup (trip_id, step, done_at, error, tried_at) values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
       on conflict (trip_id, step) do update set done_at = excluded.done_at, error = excluded.error, tried_at = excluded.tried_at`,
      [tripId, step, error === null ? o.now() : null, error, o.now()],
    );
  }

  async function run(tripId: string): Promise<void> {
    lastTry.set(tripId, o.now().getTime());
    await o.pool.query('insert into trips (trip_id, created_at) values ($1, $2) on conflict (trip_id) do nothing', [tripId, o.now()]);
    const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ step: string }>('select step from trip_setup where trip_id = $1 and done_at is not null', [tripId]);
    const done = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.step));
    for (const s of ordered()) {
      if (done.has(s.name)) continue;
      if (s.after && !done.has(s.after)) {
        await mark(tripId, s.name, `waits for ${s.after}`);
        continue;
      }
      try {
        await s.run(tripId);
        await mark(tripId, s.name, null);
        done.add(s.name);
      } catch (e) {
        await mark(tripId, s.name, (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)).slice(0, 500));
        o.log.warn({ err: e, trip: tripId, step: s.name }, 'a setup step failed; the sweep tries again');
      }
    }
    if (steps.every((s) => done.has(s.name))) ready.add(tripId);
  }

  function ensureTrip(tripId: string): Promise<void> {
    const cur = inflight.get(tripId);
    if (cur) return cur;
    const p = run(tripId).finally(() => inflight.delete(tripId));
    inflight.set(tripId, p);
    return p;
  }

  return {
    addStep(step) {
      if (steps.some((s) => s.name === step.name)) throw new Error(`setup step ${step.name} is already added`);
      steps.push(step);
      ready.clear(); // a new step is not done anywhere yet
    },
    ensureTrip,
    ensureSoon(tripId) {
      if (ready.has(tripId) || inflight.has(tripId)) return;
      const last = lastTry.get(tripId);
      if (last !== undefined && o.now().getTime() - last < retryMs) return;
      void ensureTrip(tripId).catch((e: unknown) => o.log.error({ err: e, trip: tripId }, 'setup failed'));
    },
    isReady: (tripId) => ready.has(tripId),
    async sweep() {
      if (steps.length === 0) return 0;
      const { rows } = await o.pool.query<{ trip_id: string }>(
        `select t.trip_id from trips t
         where exists (
           select 1 from unnest($1::text[]) as s(step)
           where not exists (select 1 from trip_setup ts where ts.trip_id = t.trip_id and ts.step = s.step and ts.done_at is not null))
         order by t.active_at desc limit 50`,
        [steps.map((s) => s.name)],
      );
      for (const r of rows) await ensureTrip(r.trip_id);
      return rows.length;
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/names-go.ts`:
```ts
import type { Message } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { TripPack } from './pack.js';

// "Go" spoken to, not "go" the verb: at the start ("Go, find..." but not "go to the beach"),
// at the end after a comma ("thoughts, Go?"), after a greeting ("hey go"), or @go anywhere.
const STARTS = /^\s*@?go\b(?!\s+(?:to|for|and|out|back|ahead|home|there|on|in|with|get|see|eat)\b)/i;
const ENDS = /[,!?]\s*@?go\s*[?!.]*\s*$/i;
const CALLS = /\b(?:hey|hi|hello|ok|okay|thanks|thank you|bhai|yo|dear)\s+@?go\b/i;
const AT = /(^|\s)@go\b/i;

export function namesGoInText(text: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!text) return false;
  return AT.test(text) || STARTS.test(text) || ENDS.test(text) || CALLS.test(text);
}

/** True when a message plainly names Go: the Server's mentions_go, a reply to Go, or Go spoken to in the text. */
export function plainlyNamesGo(m: Message, pack: TripPack): boolean {
  if (m.mentions_go || namesGoInText(m.text)) return true;
  if (!m.reply_to) return false;
  return pack.messages.find((x) => x.message_id === m.reply_to)?.author.kind === 'go';
}
```

`apps/brain/src/trips/screen.ts`:
```ts
import type { Message, TripEvent } from '../door/methods.js';
import { maskSecrets } from '../reflex/secrets.js';

const mask = (s: string): string => maskSecrets(s).text;

/**
 * A message with card numbers, CVVs, one-time codes and passwords hidden in everything a person typed: the text,
 * photo captions and file names. Runs before a message is written anywhere (the trip log, the messages table, the pack).
 */
export function screenMessage(m: Message): Message {
  const attachments = m.attachments.map((a) => {
    if (a.kind === 'file') return { ...a, name: mask(a.name) };
    return typeof a.caption === 'string' ? { ...a, caption: mask(a.caption) } : a;
  });
  return { ...m, ...(typeof m.text === 'string' ? { text: mask(m.text) } : {}), attachments };
}

/** The same screen on an event that carries a message; every other event passes through as it is. */
export function screenEvent(ev: TripEvent): TripEvent {
  if (ev.type === 'message.created' || ev.type === 'message.edited') {
    return { ...ev, data: { ...ev.data, message: screenMessage(ev.data.message) } };
  }
  return ev;
}
```

In `apps/brain/src/trips/pack.ts` (Task 6), add `import { screenMessage } from './screen.js';` beside the other imports and replace `pull` with this version, so messages read back from the Server (a cold load, a gap, the minute refresh) are screened before they are stored or put in the pack:
```ts
  /** Reads messages changed after `after`, page by page while has_more; screens and stores them; returns the ones Go never stored. */
  async function pull(tripId: string, after: number, pack: TripPack): Promise<Message[]> {
    const fresh: Message[] = [];
    let cursor = after;
    for (let page = 0; page < 50; page++) {
      const r = await o.link.request('read_messages', { trip_id: tripId, after_seq: cursor, limit: 200 });
      for (const raw of r.messages) {
        const m = screenMessage(raw);
        if (await storeMessage(o.pool, tripId, m, o.now())) fresh.push(m);
        upsertMessage(pack, m);
        cursor = Math.max(cursor, m.updated_seq);
      }
      if (!r.has_more || r.messages.length === 0) break;
    }
    return fresh;
  }
```

```ts
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { Speak } from '../composer/speak.js';
import type { Desk } from '../desk/desk.js';
import type { ToGoHandler } from '../door/handlers.js';
import type { Message, TripEvent } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { Log } from '../log.js';
import { UNSURE_READ, type Reflex, type ReflexRead } from '../reflex/reflex.js';
import type { HooksImpl } from './hooks.js';
import type { TripLogImpl } from './log.js';
import { namesGoInText, plainlyNamesGo } from './names-go.js';
import type { PacksImpl } from './pack.js';
import { screenEvent } from './screen.js';
import type { SetupImpl } from './setup.js';

export interface IntakeDeps {
  pool: pg.Pool;
  log: Log;
  now: () => Date;
  tripLog: TripLogImpl;
  packs: PacksImpl;
  setup: SetupImpl;
  desk: Desk;
  speak: Speak;
  hooks: HooksImpl;
  /** Read at call time: P2 replaces brain.reflex after boot wiring. */
  reflex: () => Reflex;
  runningTasks: (tripId: string) => Promise<Array<{ id: string; brief: string }>>;
  /** The wait after a message for Go, unless the reflex is sure the thought is complete. */
  waitMs?: number;
}

export function createIntake(d: IntakeDeps) {
  const waitMs = d.waitMs ?? 2_000;
  const queues = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
  const processed = new Map<string, number>();
  const handled = new Set<string>();

  /** One trip's events are handled one at a time, in the order they arrived (the Server sends them in seq order). */
  function enqueue(tripId: string, job: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
    const next = (queues.get(tripId) ?? Promise.resolve())
      .then(job)
      .catch((e: unknown) => d.log.error({ err: e, trip: tripId }, 'handling a trip event failed'));
    queues.set(tripId, next);
    void next.then(() => {
      if (queues.get(tripId) === next) queues.delete(tripId);
    });
    return next;
  }

  async function processedSeq(tripId: string): Promise<number> {
    const hit = processed.get(tripId);
    if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
    const { rows } = await d.pool.query<{ processed_seq: number }>('select processed_seq from trips where trip_id = $1', [tripId]);
    const v = rows[0]?.processed_seq ?? 0;
    processed.set(tripId, v);
    return v;
  }

  async function setProcessed(tripId: string, seq: number): Promise<void> {
    processed.set(tripId, Math.max(seq, processed.get(tripId) ?? 0));
    await d.pool.query('update trips set processed_seq = greatest(processed_seq, $2) where trip_id = $1', [tripId, seq]);
  }

  function remember(messageId: string): void {
    handled.add(messageId);
    if (handled.size > 10_000) {
      const oldest = handled.values().next().value;
      if (oldest !== undefined) handled.delete(oldest);
    }
  }

  /** A person's message: typing at once if it names Go, then the reflex, the lanes' hooks, and the desk. */
  async function onMessage(tripId: string, m: Message): Promise<void> {
    if (m.author.kind !== 'person' || m.withheld || m.deleted) return;
    const key = `${tripId}:${m.message_id}`;
    if (handled.has(key)) return;
    if (m.mentions_go || namesGoInText(m.text)) d.speak.typingOn(tripId);
    const pack = await d.packs.get(tripId);
    const namesGo = plainlyNamesGo(m, pack);
    if (namesGo && !(m.mentions_go || namesGoInText(m.text))) d.speak.typingOn(tripId); // a reply to Go
    let read: ReflexRead;
    try {
      read = await d.reflex().read(m, { pack, runningTasks: await d.runningTasks(tripId).catch(() => []) });
    } catch (e) {
      d.log.warn({ err: e, message: m.message_id }, 'the reflex failed; the composer decides');
      read = UNSURE_READ;
    }
    await d.hooks.run(m, read, pack);
    // Remembered only once read: if anything above failed, the next gap check or a restart handles it again.
    remember(key);
    // Sure chatter never wakes the model, but a message that names Go always does.
    if (!namesGo && read.forGo.sure && read.forGo.value === false) return;
    const now = d.now();
    const complete = read.complete.sure && read.complete.value === true;
    await d.desk.put({
      id: `msg:${tripId}:${m.message_id}`,
      tripId,
      kind: 'message',
      ref: m.message_id,
      dueAt: complete ? now : new Date(now.getTime() + waitMs),
      payload: { messageId: m.message_id, seq: m.seq, namesGo },
    });
  }

  /** P1's own reactions to an event; lanes add theirs with tripLog.onEvent. */
  async function react(ev: TripEvent): Promise<void> {
    if (ev.type === 'message.created') {
      await onMessage(ev.trip_id, ev.data.message);
      return;
    }
    if (ev.type === 'message.deleted') {
      await d.desk.cancel(ev.trip_id, ev.data.message_id);
      return;
    }
    if (ev.type === 'decision.closed' && ev.data.reason !== 'closed_by_go') {
      const decisionId = ev.data.decision.decision_id;
      await d.desk.put({
        id: `change:${ev.event_id}`,
        tripId: ev.trip_id,
        kind: 'trip_change',
        ref: `decision:${decisionId}`,
        dueAt: d.now(),
        payload: { type: ev.type, decisionId, reason: ev.data.reason },
      });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Holding change `from` and receiving `to` > from + 1: read the trip and every message after `from`, then
   * handle each message posted in between that this copy has not handled yet (missed, or stored by an event whose
   * handling failed).
   */
  async function fillGap(tripId: string, from: number, to: number): Promise<void> {
    d.log.info({ trip: tripId, from, to }, 'missed events: reading the trip again');
    await d.packs.refill(tripId, from);
    const pack = await d.packs.get(tripId);
    for (const m of pack.messages.filter((x) => x.seq > from && x.seq < to)) await onMessage(tripId, m);
  }

  async function processEvent(ev: TripEvent): Promise<void> {
    const tripId = ev.trip_id;
    const before = await processedSeq(tripId);
    if (ev.seq <= before) return;
    if (ev.type !== 'trip.created' && before > 0 && ev.seq > before + 1) await fillGap(tripId, before, ev.seq);
    await d.packs.apply(ev);
    // Setup runs on trip.created and on any event for a trip not set up yet; it never holds up the events.
    d.setup.ensureSoon(tripId);
    await react(ev);
    await d.tripLog.emit(ev);
    await setProcessed(tripId, ev.seq);
  }

  const sendEvent: ToGoHandler<'send_event'> = async (raw) => {
    // People's text is screened before it is written anywhere: from here on, every copy holds the masked text.
    const ev = screenEvent(raw);
    const r = await d.tripLog.append(ev);
    // Answered as soon as the event is written; the work happens after, in seq order per trip.
    if (r === 'new') void enqueue(ev.trip_id, () => processEvent(ev));
    return { accepted: true, duplicate: r === 'duplicate' };
  };

  return {
    sendEvent,
    /** On boot: events written but never processed (a crash between the answer and the work). */
    async recover(): Promise<number> {
      const { rows } = await d.pool.query<{ payload: TripEvent }>(
        `select e.payload from trip_events e join trips t on t.trip_id = e.trip_id
         where e.seq > t.processed_seq order by e.trip_id, e.seq`,
      );
      for (const r of rows) void enqueue(r.payload.trip_id, () => processEvent(r.payload));
      return rows.length;
    },
    /** Once a minute: read every active trip again, as a net for anything both sides missed. */
    async refreshActive(): Promise<void> {
      for (const tripId of d.packs.active()) {
        void enqueue(tripId, async () => {
          const fresh = await d.packs.refill(tripId);
          for (const m of fresh.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)) await onMessage(tripId, m);
        });
      }
    },
    /** Waits until every trip's queue is empty (tests and shutdown). */
    async idle(): Promise<void> {
      while (queues.size) await Promise.all([...queues.values()]);
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildBrain, type Built } from '../app.js';
import { withSchema } from '../db/test-db.js';
import { loadEnv } from '../env.js';
import { registerRoom } from '../room.js';
import { fakeAnthropic } from './fixtures/fake-anthropic.js';
import { goaSnapshot } from './fixtures/goa.js';
import { rawServer, type RawServer } from './fixtures/raw-server.js';

const SECRET = 'b'.repeat(64);
let db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
let built: Built;
let port: number;
let server: RawServer;

const model = fakeAnthropic((body) => {
  const last = body.messages.at(-1);
  const afterTools = Array.isArray(last.content) && last.content.some((b: { type: string }) => b.type === 'tool_result');
  return afterTools
    ? { text: 'Hi all, I am Go!' }
    : { tools: [{ name: 'open_vote', input: { question: 'Where should we stay?', options: [{ label: 'North Goa' }, { label: 'South Goa' }] } }] };
});

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await withSchema();
  const env = loadEnv({
    PORT: '0', DATABASE_URL: process.env.DATABASE_URL, GO_SIGNING_SECRET: SECRET, GO_PUBLIC_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1',
    ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: 'test-key', LOG_LEVEL: 'silent',
  });
  built = await buildBrain(env, { pool: db.pool, anthropic: model.client, logger: false, timings: { deskPollMs: 50 } });
  await registerRoom(built.brain);
  ({ port } = await built.start());
  server = await rawServer(`ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/v1/socket?instance=boot-test`, SECRET, (method, params) => {
    if (method === 'open_vote') return { decision_id: 'd_first', seq: 2 };
    if (method === 'post_message') return { message_id: 'm_go_1', seq: 3 };
    if (method === 'read_trip') return goaSnapshot({ trip_id: params.trip_id });
    if (method === 'read_messages') return { messages: [], has_more: false };
    throw { code: -32601, message: `no ${method} here` };
  });
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await server.close();
  await built.stop();
  await db.drop();
});

describe('boot', () => {
  it('serves /v1/health', async () => {
    const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/v1/health`);
    expect(await r.json()).toEqual({ ok: true, contract: '1.0.0' });
  });

  it('hands every lane the model client it was given, so side passes use the same fake or recorder', async () => {
    expect(built.brain.anthropic).toBe(model.client);
    const before = model.sideBodies.length;
    await built.brain.anthropic.messages.create({ model: built.brain.env.SIDE_MODEL, max_tokens: 10, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }] });
    expect(model.sideBodies.length).toBe(before + 1);
  });

  it('a new trip on the socket: accepted at once, then hello with the first vote', async () => {
    const answer = await server.call('send_event', {
      event_id: 'ev_boot_1', trip_id: 't_boot', seq: 1, occurred_at: '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z', type: 'trip.created',
      data: { trip: goaSnapshot({ trip_id: 't_boot' }) },
    });
    expect(answer.result).toEqual({ accepted: true, duplicate: false });
    const post = await server.waitFor((f) => f.method === 'post_message', 10_000);
    expect(post.id).toMatch(/^go_/);
    expect(post.params).toMatchObject({ trip_id: 't_boot', text: 'Hi all, I am Go!', card: { kind: 'vote', decision_id: 'd_first' } });
    expect(server.frames.findIndex((f) => f.method === 'open_vote')).toBeLessThan(server.frames.indexOf(post));
  });

  it('a tap on a card Go does not know is refused kindly', async () => {
    const a = await server.call('card_tap', { tap_id: 'tap_boot_1', trip_id: 't_boot', card_id: 'c_nope', action_id: 'watch', person_id: 'p_priya' });
    expect(a.result).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That card is not active any more.' });
  });

  it('each lane registers once: a second registerRoom is refused', () => {
    expect(() => registerRoom(built.brain)).toThrow(/already/);
  });
});

describe('stopping', () => {
  it('runs the lanes\' stoppers in reverse order, each awaited, and one that throws does not stop the rest', async () => {
    // A second Go on the same schema, never started: only its stop() is under test.
    const other = await buildBrain(built.brain.env, { pool: db.pool, anthropic: model.client, logger: false });
    const ran: string[] = [];
    other.brain.onStop(() => {
      ran.push('first');
    });
    other.brain.onStop(async () => {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
      ran.push('second');
    });
    other.brain.onStop(() => {
      throw new Error('this lane could not stop');
    });
    other.brain.onStop(() => {
      ran.push('fourth');
    });
    await expect(other.stop()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    // 'second' before 'first' shows each stopper is awaited before the next one runs.
    expect(ran).toEqual(['fourth', 'second', 'first']);
    await other.stop();
    expect(ran).toEqual(['fourth', 'second', 'first']);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * Lane B (P2) owns this file. P1 creates it so Brain compiles; P2 implements Memory elsewhere in memory/
 * and sets brain.memory.
 */
export interface Memory {
  /** Drawn from facts: this trip's, plus other trips' only when the person turned carry-over on. One line per fact with its proof. */
  page(tripId: string, personId: string, audience: 'group' | 'go'): Promise<string>;
  recall(tripId: string, question: string, audience: 'group' | 'go'): Promise<Array<{ text: string; messageId: string; seq: number }>>;
  /** A suggestion; code checks the proof before it becomes a fact. `topic` is the contract's Fact.topic. */
  note(
    tripId: string,
    input: { personId: string; topic: string; text: string; proofMessageId: string; audience: 'group' | 'go' },
  ): Promise<'saved' | 'refused'>;
}
```
