import type * as C from '@go/contract';

/** Lane C (P4) owns this file. P1 creates it so Brain compiles; P4 replaces the four shapes below with its own. */
export type TravelQuery = Record<string, unknown>;
export type TravelOption = Record<string, unknown>;
export type StatusQuery = Record<string, unknown>;
export type StatusResult = Record<string, unknown>;

/** One interface, two backends (ixigo MCP, fake). */
export interface Travel {
  search(kind: 'flights' | 'trains' | 'buses' | 'stays', q: TravelQuery): Promise<{ results: TravelOption[]; checkedAt: string }>;
  status(q: StatusQuery): Promise<StatusResult>;
  /** The offer behind an options card's option_id (which is also a vote option's option_ref); offerId null when the option came
   *  from a task's web browsing (P5 refuses to book those). */
  offer(optionId: string): Promise<{ offerId: string | null; title: string; priceMinor: number | null; currency: string | null } | null>;
  /** Booking is two steps so card details only ever travel between the vault and the rail. Composer and P5 only. */
  hold(offerId: string, lead: { name: string; personIds: string[] }): Promise<Hold>;
  /** Safe to repeat: the same hold confirmed twice gives the same answer. */
  confirm(holdId: string, paid: { approvalId: string; railRef: string }): Promise<Confirmed>;
}

export interface Hold {
  holdId: string;
  amountMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  merchant: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  refundableUntil?: string;
  payment: { kind: 'fake' } | { kind: 'stripe_test' } | { kind: 'ixigo'; token: string };
}

export interface Confirmed {
  ref: string;
  vendor: string;
  title: string;
  kind: C.Booking['kind'];
  startsAt?: string;
  endsAt?: string;
  refundableUntil?: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  place?: C.Place;
  journey?: C.Journey;
}

```ts
import Fastify, { type FastifyBaseLogger, type FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type pg from 'pg';
import { createSwitches, type Switches } from './admin/switches.js';
import { anthropicClient, type AnthropicLike } from './composer/anthropic.js';
import { createContext, type Context } from './composer/context.js';
import { createSpeak } from './composer/speak.js';
import { createTools, type Tools } from './composer/tools.js';
import { createComposer } from './composer/turn.js';
import { migrate } from './db/migrate.js';
import { createPool } from './db/pool.js';
import { createDesk, type Desk, type TurnRunner } from './desk/desk.js';
import { createCardStore } from './door/cards.js';
import { createDoor } from './door/door.js';
import type { Door } from './door/handlers.js';
import { createReplayGuard } from './door/replay-guard.js';
import { createServerLink } from './door/server-link.js';
import { createTapRouter, type TapRouter } from './door/taps.js';
import { newUlid } from './door/ulid.js';
import type { Env } from './env.js';
import type { Memory } from './memory/memory.js';
import type { Pay } from './money/pay.js';
import { unsureReflex, type Reflex } from './reflex/reflex.js';
import type { Tasks } from './tasks/tasks.js';
import type { Travel } from './travel/travel.js';
import { createMessageHooks, type MessageHooks } from './trips/hooks.js';
import { createIntake } from './trips/intake.js';
import { createTripLog, type TripLog } from './trips/log.js';
import { createPacks, type Packs } from './trips/pack.js';
import { createSetup, type Setup } from './trips/setup.js';

/**
 * What every lane receives at boot. main.ts calls the lanes in this order: registerRoom, registerReflex,
 * registerMemory, registerStats, registerTravel, registerTasks, registerMoney, registerProactive,
 * registerCalls, registerAdmin. A lane that has not landed yet is simply not called.
 */
export interface Brain {
  env: Env;
  pool: pg.Pool;
  log: FastifyBaseLogger;
  now: () => Date;
  http: FastifyInstance;
  /** The model client buildBrain was given; every lane's side model uses it (so tests and the bench inject a fake). */
  anthropic: AnthropicLike;
  /**
   * Lanes stop their timers, streams and sockets here. `built.stop()` runs every stopper in reverse order of
   * registration (each awaited; one that throws is logged and the rest still run), then closes the server and pool.
   */
  onStop(fn: () => Promise<void> | void): void;
  door: Door;
  taps: TapRouter;
  tripLog: TripLog;
  packs: Packs;
  setup: Setup;
  desk: Desk;
  tools: Tools;
  context: Context;
  hooks: MessageHooks;
  switches: Switches;
  /** P1 sets an all-unsure stub; P2 replaces it. */
  reflex: Reflex;
  /** Set by P2. */
  memory?: Memory;
  /** Set by P4. */
  travel?: Travel;
  /** Set by P4. */
  tasks?: Tasks;
  /** Set by P5. */
  pay?: Pay;
}

export type RegisterLane = (brain: Brain) => void | Promise<void>;

export interface Timings {
  pingMs: number;
  idleMs: number;
  /** The wait after a message for Go. */
  waitMs: number;
  deskPollMs: number;
  deskRetryMs: number[];
  leaseMs: number;
  typingEveryMs: number;
  setupSweepMs: number;
  /** The once-a-minute re-read of active trips. */
  refreshMs: number;
  pruneMs: number;
  turnTimeoutMs: number;
  linkTimeoutMs: number;
  waitForSocketMs: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_TIMINGS: Timings = {
  pingMs: 20_000,
  idleMs: 40_000,
  waitMs: 2_000,
  deskPollMs: 1_000,
  deskRetryMs: [1_000, 4_000],
  leaseMs: 60_000,
  typingEveryMs: 5_000,
  setupSweepMs: 120_000,
  refreshMs: 60_000,
  pruneMs: 60_000,
  turnTimeoutMs: 20_000,
  linkTimeoutMs: 2_500,
  waitForSocketMs: 15_000,
};

export interface BuildOptions {
  /** Tests pass a pool on their own schema; otherwise one is made from DATABASE_URL. */
  pool?: pg.Pool;
  /** Tests pass a scripted model; otherwise the real client with ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. */
  anthropic?: AnthropicLike;
  now?: () => Date;
  logger?: boolean;
  /** Tests wrap the composer's turn to make it crash at a chosen moment. */
  wrapTurn?: (run: TurnRunner) => TurnRunner;
  timings?: Partial<Timings>;
}

export interface Built {
  brain: Brain;
  start(): Promise<{ port: number }>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}

export async function buildBrain(env: Env, o: BuildOptions = {}): Promise<Built> {
  const now = o.now ?? (() => new Date());
  const t: Timings = { ...DEFAULT_TIMINGS, ...o.timings };
  const http = Fastify({ logger: o.logger === false ? false : { level: env.LOG_LEVEL } });
  const log = http.log;
  const pool = o.pool ?? createPool(env.DATABASE_URL);
  await migrate(pool);

  const cards = createCardStore({ pool, now });
  const link = createServerLink({ pool, log, now, timeoutMs: t.linkTimeoutMs, waitForSocketMs: t.waitForSocketMs, onResult: cards.observe });
  const guard = createReplayGuard({ pool, now });
  const door = createDoor({ http, secret: env.GO_SIGNING_SECRET, guard, link, log, now, pingMs: t.pingMs, idleMs: t.idleMs });
  const taps = createTapRouter({ pool, cards, log });
  const tripLog = createTripLog({ pool, log, now });
  const packs = createPacks({ pool, link, log, now });
  const hooks = createMessageHooks(log);
  const setup = createSetup({ pool, log, now });
  const desk = createDesk({
    pool, log, now, holder: `brain-${process.pid}-${newUlid()}`, pollMs: t.deskPollMs, leaseMs: t.leaseMs, retryDelaysMs: t.deskRetryMs,
  });
  const tools = createTools();
  const context = createContext(log);
  const speak = createSpeak({ pool, link, now, everyMs: t.typingEveryMs });
  const switches = createSwitches({
    pool, now, env: { TRAVEL_MODE: env.TRAVEL_MODE, PAY_RAIL: env.PAY_RAIL, CALLS_MODE: env.CALLS_MODE, REFLEX_LIVE: env.REFLEX_LIVE },
  });

  // One model client for the composer and every lane's side passes: a test's fake or the bench's recorder reaches all of them.
  const anthropic = o.anthropic ?? anthropicClient(env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY);
  // What stop() runs, newest first: lanes register after buildBrain returns, so their timers stop while the room is still up.
  const stoppers: Array<() => Promise<void> | void> = [];
  const onStop = (fn: () => Promise<void> | void): void => {
    stoppers.push(fn);
  };
  const brain: Brain = {
    env, pool, log, now, http, anthropic, onStop, door, taps, tripLog, packs, setup, desk, tools, context, hooks, switches, reflex: unsureReflex,
  };

  const composer = createComposer({
    pool, log, now, link, packs, tools, context, speak,
    client: anthropic,
    model: env.COMPOSER_MODEL,
    claimNew: desk.claimNew,
    turnTimeoutMs: t.turnTimeoutMs,
  });
  desk.setRunner(o.wrapTurn ? o.wrapTurn(composer.runTurn) : composer.runTurn, composer.giveUp);

  // send_event and card_tap need the trip log, packs and setup internals, so they are wired here, not in a lane.
  const intake = createIntake({
    pool, log, now, tripLog, packs, setup, desk, speak, hooks,
    reflex: () => brain.reflex,
    runningTasks: async (tripId) => (await brain.tasks?.running(tripId)) ?? [],
    waitMs: t.waitMs,
  });
  door.handle('send_event', intake.sendEvent);
  door.handle('card_tap', taps.handle);

  // P1's own stops go through the same rail as every lane's, registered first so they run last:
  // the setup sweep, the minute refresh and the prune, then the link, the desk poller and its turns, then typing.
  const timers: NodeJS.Timeout[] = [];
  onStop(() => speak.stopAll());
  onStop(() => desk.stop());
  onStop(() => link.close());
  onStop(() => {
    for (const x of timers) clearInterval(x);
  });

  let listening = false;
  return {
    brain,
    async start() {
      await switches.refresh();
      await http.listen({ port: env.PORT, host: '0.0.0.0' });
      listening = true;
      desk.start();
      await intake.recover();
      void link.resumePending().catch((e: unknown) => log.warn({ err: e }, 'resending writes after a restart failed'));
      // unref: these never keep the process alive on their own; the stopper above clears them.
      timers.push(
        setInterval(() => void setup.sweep().catch((e: unknown) => log.warn({ err: e }, 'the setup sweep failed')), t.setupSweepMs).unref(),
        setInterval(() => void intake.refreshActive().catch((e: unknown) => log.warn({ err: e }, 'the trip refresh failed')), t.refreshMs).unref(),
        setInterval(() => void guard.prune().catch((e: unknown) => log.warn({ err: e }, 'pruning signatures failed')), t.pruneMs).unref(),
      );
      const addr = http.server.address();
      return { port: typeof addr === 'object' && addr ? addr.port : env.PORT };
    },
    async stop() {
      // Newest first. splice(0) empties the list, so a second stop() runs nothing twice.
      for (const fn of stoppers.splice(0).reverse()) {
        try {
          await fn();
        } catch (e) {
          log.warn({ err: e }, 'a stopper failed; stopping the rest');
        }
      }
      door.close();
      if (listening) await http.close();
      await intake.idle();
      if (!o.pool) await pool.end();
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/room.ts`:
```ts
import type { RegisterLane } from './app.js';
import { roomTools } from './composer/room-tools.js';
import { tripNow } from './composer/trip-now.js';
import { renderMessageWake, renderSetupWake, renderTripChangeWake } from './composer/wakes.js';

/** Lane A's own contributions, made through the same interfaces every lane uses. */
export const registerRoom: RegisterLane = (brain) => {
  for (const t of roomTools({ now: brain.now })) brain.tools.register(t);
  brain.context.addWake('message', renderMessageWake);
  brain.context.addWake('trip_change', renderTripChangeWake);
  brain.context.addWake('setup', renderSetupWake);
  brain.context.addNow(tripNow(brain.packs));
  // Go's side of setup: the trip row and pack, then hello and the first vote, unless Go has already spoken here.
  brain.setup.addStep({
    name: 'brain.ready',
    run: async (tripId) => {
      const pack = await brain.packs.get(tripId);
      if (pack.messages.some((m) => m.author.kind === 'go')) return;
      await brain.desk.put({ id: `setup:${tripId}:greet`, tripId, kind: 'setup', ref: 'greet', dueAt: brain.now(), payload: {} });
    },
  });
};
```

`apps/brain/src/main.ts`:
```ts
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { buildBrain, type RegisterLane } from './app.js';
import { loadEnv } from './env.js';
import { registerRoom } from './room.js';

/**
 * The lanes, in boot order: registerRoom, registerReflex, registerMemory, registerStats, registerTravel,
 * registerTasks, registerMoney, registerProactive, registerCalls, registerAdmin. Each lane adds its one
 * line here, in that order, in its own PR.
 */
export const LANES: RegisterLane[] = [registerRoom];

export async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const env = loadEnv();
  const built = await buildBrain(env);
  for (const lane of LANES) await lane(built.brain);
  const { port } = await built.start();
  built.brain.log.info({ port, model: env.COMPOSER_MODEL }, 'Go is listening');
  let stopping = false;
  const shutdown = (): void => {
    if (stopping) return;
    stopping = true;
    void built.stop().finally(() => process.exit(0));
  };
  process.once('SIGTERM', shutdown);
  process.once('SIGINT', shutdown);
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  main().catch((e: unknown) => {
    console.error(e);
    process.exit(1);
  });
}
```

### Task 16: End to end with the simulator: Go greets, answers, and stays quiet

Waits for P0's `@go/sim` (P0 Task 9, the FakeServer, with `reconnect`) to be on `build`. Everything before this task is green without it.

The harness lives outside `__tests__` (`apps/brain/src/testing/`), so it is built into `dist` and every later lane's end-to-end test and P3's bench can reuse it. It registers `LANES`, every lane that has landed, so each lane that lands runs under G1's checks too: a lane whose landing turns one of these checks red fixes it in its own PR. `src/__tests__/e2e/harness.ts` re-exports it, so tests that already import from there keep working.

`apps/brain/src/testing/watch.ts`:
```ts
// What the FakeServer saw from Go, read the way the end-to-end checks and the smoke run need it: times in ms.
import type { FakeServer, WriteRecord } from '@go/sim';

/** One of Go's writes as the FakeServer saw it. `applied` is true for the arrival that changed the trip. */
export interface Seen {
  method: string;
  request_id: string;
  params: Record<string, any>;
  applied: boolean;
  /** When it arrived, in ms since the epoch. */
  at: number;
}

const seen = (w: WriteRecord): Seen => ({
  method: w.method,
  request_id: w.request_id,
  params: w.params as Record<string, any>,
  applied: w.applied,
  at: Date.parse(w.at),
});

/** Every write Go sent, repeats included, in arrival order. */
export const writesOf = (fake: FakeServer): Seen[] => fake.writes.map(seen);

/** Waits for the first write that matches. */
export function waitWrite(fake: FakeServer, pred: (w: Seen) => boolean, timeoutMs = 10_000): Promise<Seen> {
  return fake.waitFor(() => writesOf(fake).find(pred), timeoutMs);
}

/** Go's typing notifications (requests, never writes), with their arrival times in ms. */
export function typingOf(fake: FakeServer): Array<{ tripId: string; on: boolean; at: number }> {
  return fake.requests
    .filter((r) => r.method === 'typing')
    .map((r) => ({ tripId: String(r.params.trip_id), on: r.params.on === true, at: Date.parse(r.at) }));
}

/** Waits for typing on (or off) that arrived at or after `since` (ms). */
export function waitTyping(fake: FakeServer, on: boolean, since: number, timeoutMs = 2_000): Promise<{ tripId: string; on: boolean; at: number }> {
  return fake.waitFor(() => typingOf(fake).find((t) => t.on === on && t.at >= since), timeoutMs);
}

/** How many arrivals of this write changed the trip: 1 means the Server did Go's write once, however often it arrived. */
export function appliedCount(fake: FakeServer, requestId: string): number {
  return fake.writes.filter((w) => w.params.request_id === requestId && w.applied).length;
}

/** Messages Go posted (each counted once, when it was applied) whose text contains `text`. */
export function postsWith(fake: FakeServer, text: string): Seen[] {
  return writesOf(fake).filter(
    (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.applied && typeof w.params.text === 'string' && w.params.text.includes(text),
  );
}
```

`apps/brain/src/testing/start-room.ts`:
```ts
// Go on a real port with its own database schema and a given model, every landed lane registered, and @go/sim's
// FakeServer connected to it. P1's end-to-end tests use it; later lanes' end-to-end tests and P3's bench reuse it.
// It lives outside __tests__, so it is built into dist (P3 imports @go/brain/dist/testing/start-room.js).
import { FakeServer } from '@go/sim';
import { buildBrain, type Built, type RegisterLane, type Timings } from '../app.js';
import type { AnthropicLike } from '../composer/anthropic.js';
import { withSchema } from '../db/test-db.js';
import type { TurnRunner } from '../desk/desk.js';
import { loadEnv } from '../env.js';
import { LANES } from '../main.js';

export const SECRET = 'e'.repeat(64);
/** Short waits for tests; everything else runs as in production. */
export const TEST_TIMINGS: Partial<Timings> = { waitMs: 400, deskPollMs: 50, deskRetryMs: [50, 100] };

const firstUser = (body: any): Array<{ type: string; text?: string }> => {
  const content = body?.messages?.[0]?.content;
  return Array.isArray(content) ? content : [];
};
/** The newest messages of a request: the last block of its first user message. */
export const newSince = (body: any): string => String(firstUser(body).at(-1)?.text ?? '');
/** "Right now", with why Go woke: the block before it. */
export const rightNow = (body: any): string => String(firstUser(body).at(-2)?.text ?? '');
/** True when the request answers a round of tool calls. */
export const afterTools = (body: any): boolean => {
  const last = body?.messages?.at(-1);
  return Array.isArray(last?.content) && last.content.some((b: { type: string }) => b.type === 'tool_result');
};
export const sleep = (ms: number): Promise<void> => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

export interface RoomOptions {
  /** The model: a scripted fake in tests, a recording client in the bench. Every lane's side passes use it too. */
  anthropic: AnthropicLike;
  /** The lanes to register, in boot order. Default: LANES, every lane that has landed. */
  lanes?: RegisterLane[];
  /** Wraps the composer's turn, to make it crash at a chosen moment. */
  wrapTurn?: (run: TurnRunner) => TurnRunner;
  /** The FakeServer's instance name. Default "sim-1". */
  instance?: string;
  /** More environment variables, for a lane under test. */
  env?: Record<string, string>;
  timings?: Partial<Timings>;
}

export interface Room {
  fake: FakeServer;
  built: Built;
  db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof withSchema>>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}

export async function startRoom(o: RoomOptions): Promise<Room> {
  const db = await withSchema();
  const env = loadEnv({
    PORT: '0', DATABASE_URL: process.env.DATABASE_URL, GO_SIGNING_SECRET: SECRET, GO_PUBLIC_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1',
    ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: 'test-key', LOG_LEVEL: 'silent', ...o.env,
  });
  const built = await buildBrain(env, {
    pool: db.pool,
    anthropic: o.anthropic,
    logger: false,
    timings: { ...TEST_TIMINGS, ...o.timings },
    ...(o.wrapTurn ? { wrapTurn: o.wrapTurn } : {}),
  });
  for (const lane of o.lanes ?? LANES) await lane(built.brain);
  const { port } = await built.start();
  const fake = new FakeServer({ goUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`, secret: SECRET, instance: o.instance ?? 'sim-1' });
  await fake.start(5_000);
  return {
    fake,
    built,
    db,
    async stop() {
      fake.stop();
      await built.stop();
      await db.drop();
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/brain/src/__tests__/e2e/room.e2e.test.ts`:
```ts
import { goaFreshTrip } from '@go/sim';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fakeAnthropic, type Reply } from '../fixtures/fake-anthropic.js';
import {
  afterTools, appliedCount, newSince, rightNow, sleep, startRoom, typingOf, waitTyping, waitWrite, writesOf, type Room,
} from './harness.js';

function goScript(body: any): Reply {
  if (rightNow(body).includes('you just joined this trip')) {
    return afterTools(body)
      ? { text: 'Hi all, I am Go! I can find stays and flights, run votes and keep the plan. First up, where should we stay?' }
      : { tools: [{ name: 'open_vote', input: { question: 'Where should we stay?', options: [{ label: 'North Goa villa' }, { label: 'South Goa resort' }] } }] };
  }
  if (/@go/i.test(newSince(body))) return { text: 'Friday evening flights from Delhi after 6 pm, on it.' };
  return { tools: [{ name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'chatter' } }] };
}

const model = fakeAnthropic(goScript);
let room: Room;
beforeAll(async () => {
  room = await startRoom({ anthropic: model.client });
}, 30_000);
afterAll(async () => {
  await room.stop();
});

describe('G1: Go hears the room', () => {
  it('greets a new trip and opens the first vote', async () => {
    await room.fake.createTrip(goaFreshTrip());
    const vote = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'open_vote');
    expect(vote.params.question).toBe('Where should we stay?');
    const hello = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.params.card?.kind === 'vote');
    expect(hello.params.text).toMatch(/^Hi all, I am Go!/);
    expect(hello.params.text).not.toMatch(/[—–]/);
    expect(hello.request_id).toMatch(/^go_post_turn_/);
    expect(appliedCount(room.fake, hello.request_id)).toBe(1);
    expect(room.fake.messages().filter((m) => m.author.kind === 'go')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('"@Go ..." shows typing in under 0.5 s, then an answer, then typing off', async () => {
    const sentAt = Date.now();
    await room.fake.say('p_arjun', "@Go flights Delhi to Goa Friday evening? I can't leave before 6");
    const typing = await waitTyping(room.fake, true, sentAt);
    expect(typing.at - sentAt).toBeLessThan(500);
    const reply = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.at >= sentAt);
    expect(reply.params.text).toBe('Friday evening flights from Delhi after 6 pm, on it.');
    expect(appliedCount(room.fake, reply.request_id)).toBe(1);
    await waitTyping(room.fake, false, reply.at);
  });

  it('chatter gets nothing: no typing, no message', async () => {
    const sentAt = Date.now();
    await room.fake.say('p_meher', 'lol');
    await sleep(1_500);
    expect(writesOf(room.fake).filter((w) => w.at >= sentAt && w.method === 'post_message')).toEqual([]);
    expect(typingOf(room.fake).filter((t) => t.at >= sentAt && t.on)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('a tap on a card Go does not know is refused kindly', async () => {
    // FakeServer.tap only taps cards the trip shows, so a stale card goes straight to Go, as an old screen in the App would.
    const answer = await room.fake.askGo(
      'card_tap',
      { tap_id: 'tap_e2e_1', trip_id: 't_goa', card_id: 'c_unknown', action_id: 'watch', person_id: 'p_priya' },
      3_500,
    );
    expect(answer).toEqual({ result: 'refused', toast: 'That card is not active any more.' });
  });
});
```

 && pnpm --filter "@go/brain..." build
ENV NODE_ENV=production

```ts
// By hand after a deploy: plays the Server against the deployed Go, with the real model.
//   GO_URL=https://<brain host> GO_SIGNING_SECRET=... pnpm --filter @go/brain exec tsx scripts/smoke.ts
import { FakeServer, goaFreshTrip } from '@go/sim';
import { waitTyping, waitWrite } from '../src/testing/watch.js';

const goUrl = process.env.GO_URL;
const secret = process.env.GO_SIGNING_SECRET;
if (!goUrl || !secret) throw new Error('set GO_URL and GO_SIGNING_SECRET');

const fake = new FakeServer({ goUrl, secret, instance: `smoke-${Date.now()}` });
await fake.start(15_000);
await fake.createTrip({ ...goaFreshTrip(), trip_id: `t_smoke_${Date.now()}` });
const hello = await waitWrite(fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message', 30_000);
console.log('greeting:', hello.params.text, '| card:', hello.params.card?.kind);

const sentAt = Date.now();
await fake.say('p_priya', '@Go what is the weather like in Goa in early November?');
const typing = await waitTyping(fake, true, sentAt, 5_000);
const reply = await waitWrite(fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.at > sentAt, 30_000);
// Measured from this machine: includes the round trip to Railway's region.
console.log(`typing after ${typing.at - sentAt} ms, reply after ${reply.at - sentAt} ms:`, reply.params.text);
fake.stop();
```

### Task 18: End to end: a burst, two crashes and a reconnect (G1 complete)

**Files:**
- Test: `apps/brain/src/__tests__/e2e/recovery.e2e.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `startRoom`, `Room`, `newSince`, `rightNow`, `afterTools`, `sleep`, `waitWrite`, `waitTyping`, `appliedCount`, `postsWith` (Task 16, through `./harness.js`); `TurnRunner` (Task 9); `fakeAnthropic`, `Reply` (Task 12); from `@go/sim` (P0 Task 9): `say` resolving with the posted `Message`, `reconnect({ downMs })` (drops the Server's socket and dials again on a new one signed fresh, keeping every trip, write and event), the `writes` property, and `goaFreshTrip()`. "Done once" is `appliedCount(fake, id) === 1`: P0 counts every arrival in `writes` (`countWrites` includes repeats) and marks the one that changed the trip `applied`.
- Produces: the G1 gate's last four checks, in CI.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`apps/brain/src/__tests__/e2e/recovery.e2e.test.ts`:
```ts
import { goaFreshTrip } from '@go/sim';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { TurnRunner } from '../../desk/desk.js';
import { fakeAnthropic, type Reply } from '../fixtures/fake-anthropic.js';
import {
  afterTools, appliedCount, newSince, postsWith, rightNow, sleep, startRoom, waitTyping, waitWrite, type Room,
} from './harness.js';

// Messages carry a tag, [t:name], that the scripted model and the checks key on.
const tagOf = (text: string): string | null => /\[t:([a-z-]+)\]/.exec(text)?.[1] ?? null;
let failBeforePosting = 1;
let crashAfterPosting: string | null = null; // the message id whose turn dies right after it posted
let crashed = false;

function script(body: any): Reply {
  if (rightNow(body).includes('you just joined this trip')) {
    return afterTools(body)
      ? { text: 'Hi all, I am Go!' }
      : { tools: [{ name: 'open_vote', input: { question: 'Where should we stay?', options: [{ label: 'North Goa' }, { label: 'South Goa' }] } }] };
  }
  const fresh = newSince(body);
  if (fresh.includes('burst-')) return { text: `Read ${(fresh.match(/burst-\d/g) ?? []).length} new messages.` };
  const tag = tagOf(fresh);
  if (tag === 'crash-before' && failBeforePosting > 0) {
    failBeforePosting -= 1;
    return { throws: new Error('the model fell over') };
  }
  if (tag === 'slow') return { text: 'ok [t:slow]', delayMs: 2_500 };
  if (tag) return { text: `ok [t:${tag}]` };
  return { tools: [{ name: 'stay_silent', input: { reason: 'chatter' } }] };
}

const dieAfterPosting = (run: TurnRunner): TurnRunner => async (t) => {
  await run(t);
  if (!crashed && crashAfterPosting && t.items.some((i) => i.ref === crashAfterPosting)) {
    crashed = true;
    throw new Error('this copy of Go died right after posting');
  }
};

const model = fakeAnthropic(script);
let room: Room;
beforeAll(async () => {
  room = await startRoom({ anthropic: model.client, wrapTurn: dieAfterPosting });
  await room.fake.createTrip(goaFreshTrip());
  await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.params.card?.kind === 'vote');
}, 30_000);
afterAll(async () => {
  await room.stop();
});

const askedAbout = (from: number, text: string) => model.bodies.slice(from).filter((b) => newSince(b).includes(text));

describe('G1: Go holds up', () => {
  it('a burst of five gets one turn that reads all five', async () => {
    const before = model.bodies.length;
    await room.fake.say('p_rahul', 'burst-1 scuba on Sunday?');
    await room.fake.say('p_priya', 'burst-2 yes!!');
    await room.fake.say('p_arjun', 'burst-3 @Go is scuba safe in November?');
    await room.fake.say('p_meher', "burst-4 I can't swim lol");
    await room.fake.say('p_kabir', 'burst-5 snorkelling then');
    const reply = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && /^Read \d new messages\.$/.test(w.params.text ?? ''));
    expect(reply.params.text).toBe('Read 5 new messages.');
    expect(askedAbout(before, 'burst-')).toHaveLength(1);
    await sleep(800);
    expect(postsWith(room.fake, 'Read ')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('a crash before posting: the turn runs again and posts once', async () => {
    const before = model.bodies.length;
    await room.fake.say('p_arjun', '@Go [t:crash-before] is the villa pet friendly?');
    const reply = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.params.text === 'ok [t:crash-before]');
    expect(appliedCount(room.fake, reply.request_id)).toBe(1);
    expect(askedAbout(before, '[t:crash-before]')).toHaveLength(2);
    await sleep(500);
    expect(postsWith(room.fake, '[t:crash-before]')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('a crash right after posting: the rerun neither posts again nor asks the model again', async () => {
    const before = model.bodies.length;
    const m = await room.fake.say('p_priya', '@Go [t:crash-after] what time is check-in?');
    crashAfterPosting = m.message_id;
    const reply = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.params.text === 'ok [t:crash-after]');
    await sleep(800);
    expect(crashed).toBe(true);
    expect(postsWith(room.fake, '[t:crash-after]')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(appliedCount(room.fake, reply.request_id)).toBe(1);
    expect(askedAbout(before, '[t:crash-after]')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('the Server reconnects while Go is thinking: the answer goes out once, on the new socket', async () => {
    const sentAt = Date.now();
    await room.fake.say('p_kabir', '@Go [t:slow] can we check in at 10?');
    await waitTyping(room.fake, true, sentAt);
    // The Server drops its socket and dials again 1.1 s later: a new second makes a new upgrade signature
    // (Go accepts each signature once). The model answers 2.5 s after the turn began, so the post meets the new socket.
    await room.fake.reconnect({ downMs: 1_100 });
    expect(room.fake.isOpen()).toBe(true);
    const reply = await waitWrite(room.fake, (w) => w.method === 'post_message' && w.params.text === 'ok [t:slow]', 15_000);
    expect(appliedCount(room.fake, reply.request_id)).toBe(1);
    await sleep(500);
    expect(postsWith(room.fake, '[t:slow]')).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

## Interface notes for the conductor

Most of what P1 first asked for is now in master §6.3; those items say "settled in master §6.3" and stay as a record of what P1 builds. The open ones say so.

1. **`ServerLink.request` options carry `turnId?: string`.** Settled in master §6.3. `TurnCtx.link` sets it (through `scopedLink`), so `go_requests.turn_id` tells a rerun what the crashed turn already did ("already posted" skips the model). Lanes never pass it themselves.
2. **`TurnCtx` fields `cards`, `stableId(prefix)`, `signal`, `allowedTools?`, `mayPostText?`.** Settled in master §6.3. Every lane's quick tool takes its write's `request_id` from `ctx.stableId('go_<tag>')` and new card ids from `ctx.stableId('c_<kind>')`, so a rerun repeats them. The limits come from `Context.addTurnRules` (Task 10), are put on the turn by Task 13, and are enforced by the loop (Task 12): a call to any tool outside `allowedTools` except `stay_silent` gets an error result "not allowed this turn" and never runs; the tool definitions sent never change (they sit under the 1h breakpoint); with `mayPostText: false` the text is dropped and the cards in `ctx.cards` still go.
3. **Type-only seam files** `memory/memory.ts`, `travel/travel.ts`, `tasks/tasks.ts`, `money/pay.ts`. Settled in master §6.3 notes. P1's copies follow master §6.3 as it stands (`Travel.offer`, `Hold.refundableUntil?`, the idempotent `confirm`, `Tasks.handoff`); the owning lane edits them in place, adding, never renaming. `TravelQuery`, `TravelOption`, `StatusQuery`, `StatusResult` are `Record<string, unknown>` until P4 defines them. P2 owns `reflex/reflex.ts` after this plan (P1 wrote the stub there) and its own `reflex_reads` table; P1 stores no reads.
4. **`send_event` and `card_tap` are registered in `buildBrain`.** Settled in master §6.3 notes.
5. **No `reply` tool; `stay_silent` ends a turn in the same round.** Settled in master §6.3 notes. `reply_to` is set by code when others spoke after the one message that named Go. The pre-post check and the `turns` record live in `composer/turn.ts`; `composer/speak.ts` keeps typing, posting, cleaning and `posted_at`.
6. **"go" as a word.** Settled in master §6.4 step 3 (`trips/names-go.ts`). P2's Jev Q1 can replace the rule.
7. **Desk additions outside the shared `Desk`** (P1's own, no lane needs them): `TurnInput`, `TurnRunner`, `DeskImpl.claimNew` (the pre-post check), `tick`, `inProcessWake: false` for tests. The id prefixes (`msg:`, `change:`, `setup:`) are settled in master §6.3 notes.
8. **`withSchema()` returns `{ pool, schema, drop }`.** Settled in master §6.3 notes.
9. **`REFLEX_LIVE` as a switch.** Settled. P1's `SWITCH_RULES.REFLEX_LIVE` (Task 2) accepts `off` (no Jev call at all, P2's kill switch), `q1,q2,...` or empty, the same pattern as P2's `ReflexEnv`; P2 does not edit `admin/switches.ts`.
10. **After a gap, lanes see only the events that arrived.** P1 refreshes the pack from `read_trip` and wakes for missed messages; a lane that needs exact history reads the pack, not every event through `TripLog.onEvent`.
11. **Open: `trips.tz`** defaults to `Asia/Kolkata`; the contract's `TripSnapshot` has no time zone, so nothing sets it yet. P6's night-time bar may need a source.
12. **`@go/sim`: settled against P0 Task 9's real FakeServer.** Tasks 16 to 18, `startRoom` and `smoke.ts` use `new FakeServer({ goUrl, secret, instance? })`, `start(timeoutMs?)`, `stop()`, `isOpen()`, `createTrip(snapshot): Promise<SentEvent>`, `say(personId, text, opts?): Promise<Message>`, `askGo(method, params, timeoutMs?)` (for a tap on a card the trip does not show; `tap(cardId, actionId, personId, optionId?)` only taps cards the trip shows), `waitFor(check, timeoutMs?)`, `messages()`, the properties `writes: WriteRecord[]` (one per arrival, `at` an ISO string, `applied` on the arrival that changed the trip) and `requests` (every request and typing notification), and `goaFreshTrip()`. "Done once" is `appliedCount(fake, id)`, which is `fake.writes.filter((w) => w.params.request_id === id && w.applied).length === 1`; `countWrites(id)` counts arrivals, repeats included. Two things P1 needed were added to P0 Task 9 with their own tests: `reconnect({ downMs?, timeoutMs? })` (drops the socket and dials again on a new one, signed fresh, keeping every trip, write and event; a waiting event goes again with its `event_id`), and a check that typing notifications land in `requests` with their arrival time.
13. **`Door.onUpgrade(path: RegExp, handle)`.** Settled in master §6.3. Checked before the `/v1/socket` rule, with no signature check by the door; a pattern that would match `/v1/socket` is refused when it is registered. P6's fallback to `brain.http.server.on('upgrade')` never runs once this lands.
14. **`Context.addWake` (a list per kind), `setStory`, `addTurnRules`.** Settled in master §6.3. For P2: `setStory` takes one provider and throws on a second call; the story is fenced with `defuse` (P2's lines already turn `<` and `>` into `‹›`, so nothing changes for them); breakpoint 2 stays on the last full chunk, or on the story while no chunk is full. For P6: a rule returns `{ tools, mayPostText }` (master's names; P6's note 2 used `allowedTools`), so P6 can pass `rulesForTurn` straight to `addTurnRules`, and the tools it returns must include the read tools it wants (`trip`, `person`, `recall`; P6's `ALWAYS` already adds them). A rule that throws is skipped with a warning.
15. **`Brain.anthropic: AnthropicLike` with `messages.stream` and `messages.create`.** Settled in master §6.3. P2's `registerMemory` (P2 Task 15) builds its side model with `anthropicSideModel(brain.anthropic, env.SIDE_MODEL, log)`; nothing in P2 calls `new Anthropic(`.
16. **People's text screened at the door.** Settled in master §6.3 notes. P2 Task 1 (`reflex/secrets.ts`) is built by the P1 builder right after P1 Task 6, exactly as P2's plan writes it; P2's builder skips it. `trips/screen.ts` masks the text, photo captions and file names of every message before `trip_events`, and `pull` masks what `read_messages` returns.
17. **Every lane's env stub.** Settled in master §6.3 notes and §6.7. P2 Task 2 modifies `reflex/env.ts` and P2 Task 15 modifies `memory/env.ts` (P1's stubs; neither touches `env.ts`); P3 declares no Go variable, so `stats/env.ts` stays empty. A lane that fills its stub adds its variables to `apps/brain/.env.example` in the same task (stated in Tasks 2 and 17; Task 17's test checks every key of `Env`).
18. **The end-to-end harness is in `src/testing/` and registers `LANES`.** `startRoom({ anthropic, lanes?, wrapTurn?, instance?, env?, timings? })` and the `watch.ts` readers are outside `__tests__`, so they are built into `dist` (P3's bench imports `@go/brain/dist/testing/start-room.js`). `src/__tests__/e2e/harness.ts` re-exports both, so P4's and P6's imports of `SECRET`, `TEST_TIMINGS`, `rightNow`, `newSince`, `afterTools` from there work unchanged; P5's own `startMoneyRoom` can switch to `startRoom`. Settled: master §6.2 has the `testing/` row (owner P1), and master §6.3 has `Brain.onStop(fn)` (Task 15), so `room.stop()` stops every lane's timers through `built.stop()`.
19. **`open_vote` passes every `DecisionOption` field.** Settled in master §6.3 notes. The tool's option schema is the contract's `DecisionOption` copied by shape (so the tool's JSON schema has no `$ref`), and every field the model filled goes through; null ones are left out.
20. **Trip affinity in `ServerLink`.** Settled in master §6.3. The door calls `link.noteTrip(tripId, socketId)` for every `send_event` before answering it (Task 5); a request or `typing` for that trip goes on that socket while it is open, and on any open socket when no event has come yet or that socket closed (a write lost with its socket is resent there, the same JSON-RPC id and `request_id`). A request with no `trip_id` (none in contract 1.0.0) uses any open socket. Rehearsals never use it to share a Go: by master §9 the rehearsal simulator connects only to `brain-rehearsal`, never the stage Go, so affinity only keeps several Server instances apart and the edge below cannot happen on stage. One edge stays: after the trip's socket closes, a late write for that trip falls back to any open socket, so a rehearsal trip's last card update after the simulator left can reach the real Server, which answers 404 (final, nothing retried or changed there).
21. **`TapRouter.dispatch(tap)`.** Settled in master §6.3. It shares `handle`'s once-per-`tap_id` path (Task 8), so P7's "Decline the waiting pay card" calls `brain.taps.dispatch(...)` with no cast; `Brain.taps` stays typed as master's `TapRouter`, and `handle` stays on `TapRouterImpl` for the door.

## Self-review

**Spec coverage**

| Spec section | Where |
|---|---|
| §2 one service, its own Postgres, Railway in the Server's region | Task 1 (database, CI Postgres), Task 15 (boot), Task 17 (Railway, region step) |
| §3.1 signed upgrade, each signature once, `?instance=` | Task 5 (`replay-guard.ts`, upgrade check, exact target) |
| §3.2 answer limits, `accepted` before any thinking, unknown types first | Task 5 (limits, unknown type), Task 14 (`sendEvent` answers after the write) |
| §3.3 every event once by `event_id`, then the desk | Task 6 (`TripLog.append`), Task 14 (processing, desk items) |
| §3.4 gaps: `read_trip` + `read_messages`, paging; minute re-read | Task 6 (`refill`), Task 14 (gap test, `refreshActive`) |
| §3.5 Go's requests on any socket, `go_` ids, resend with the same ids; master §6.3 trip affinity | Task 4 (ServerLink, reconnect and restart tests; "a trip's requests and typing on the socket that last brought its events", "a trip's socket closes...", "a trip no socket has brought yet..."), Task 5 ("sends Go's requests for a trip back on the socket that brought that trip's events") |
| §3.6 ping 20 s, close after 40 s | Task 5 (ping and idle tests) |
